If the RPC interface itself moved (method names / type-ids), the `cronometer-mcp` PyPI package tracks it upstream.
- **Actions minutes**: Repo is private; hourly schedule is estimated to use ~3000–6000 min/month, likely over the 2000-min free tier. Monitor usage under GitHub → Settings → Billing.

## Conventions

- After making frontend changes, always start the dev server (`cd frontend && npm run dev`) so the user can see updates immediately in the browser
- After completing a task, always commit and push to git — Vercel auto-deploys from `master` (root directory: `frontend`)
- **Speed budget (a standing requirement, restated by Riley 2026-11-14): nothing Riley waits for takes a full second.** Every page shows something the instant it is clicked (`loading.tsx` for anything not ISR), data is preloaded in the background when the app opens and served from the client cache, and no API or database call on a page takes 1 s. `perf-timing.yml` samples production hourly (not per deployment) and goes red on a breach (`perf-report.cjs --budget`, `budgetViolations` in `e2e/perf-stats.cjs`): any single API / database / Worker call of 1 s or more (the slowest call per view, not a mean), a page's data taking 1 s in any warm view or on the cold first visit, or a page not measured completely (a missing navigation is never a pass). It covers full loads of Today (every panel), Log (every section with its data), Check-in, the dashboards, Status, Financials, Budgets and Cash forecast, plus in-app navigations clicked from the sidebar after Today settles; each breach is labelled repeatable or isolated. The cloud watchdog raises a red run as `speed-budget-broken`; a red run is fixed in the next PR, not reported for later. Every frontend change states its before/after timing. A page's first GETs can start with the HTML instead of after the JavaScript and hydration (`lib/early-fetch.ts`: an inline script starts them, the client takes each response once for the same URL within 10 s; a 401, a redirect to sign-in or a network error is fetched again by the client so session-end handling still sees it). Used by `/check-in`, `/status` (its one read, same cache mode as the page's own fetch) and Today (`/` and `/today`: `app/today/EarlyReads.tsx` + `lib/today-early.ts` start reading, urgent email, orders, renewals, money alerts, the calendar for local today + tomorrow and the agents Worker's inbox with the session's token read from the Supabase cookie, and preconnect to the Worker) and `/agents` (`app/agents/EarlyInbox.tsx`: the inbox, or, when the URL opens one conversation with `?t=`, that conversation's first 60 messages, which AgentsChat takes once) and `/log` (`app/log/EarlyReads.tsx` + `lib/log-early.ts`: the check-in, supplements today / stacks / products, weight, reading, behavioral today and the journal list for the New York date; each section takes its answer through `cachedJson(url, { get: logEarlyGet(url, routed) })`, the embedded check-in through `earlyRead`; the Uber Eats section's Supabase reads, which a plain script cannot make, start when LogLoader's module runs after a full load, `app/log/early-delivery.ts`). GET only and read-only: never `/api/experiments/today` (it stamps the arm as revealed), never anything that writes or marks read; only the routed page takes them, never its copy in the continuous scroll. A URL added there must equal the client's own (`tests/early-fetch.test.cjs` checks Today's and /log's).
- **Input saves itself (Riley, 2026-11-15: "Onyx should autosave when I input data").** No Save buttons for data entry: use `lib/autosave.ts` (`useAutosave` in `lib/use-autosave.ts`, status line `components/AutosaveStatus.tsx`, `saveRequest` for the fetch). A tap saves after a short delay (a burst of taps is one request), typing after a pause and at once on Enter / blur / page hidden (keepalive) / unmount; one request in flight, the newest value wins; a 4xx (`AutosaveRefused`) is shown with Retry and not retried, anything else retries with backoff (a form whose server can tell a failure that may have been stored anyway passes `unknownOutcome`: until a save answers, changing back to the last saved value is sent, not dropped as unchanged); everything not saved yet counts as unsaved work, so the updater never reloads over it. A new record is created once its required fields are complete and later edits UPDATE that record (never a second create). What the Say it box fills is Riley's own words and saves the same way (a journal entry the model only guessed at waits for Keep). Still never on open or on scroll (`useInFeed`). An explicit button stays only where the tap IS the action (Log a dose, Log it for a stack, Record payment), for a batch with no update path (lab results), or where a half-finished record must not land (photo-to-product review); say why in the code. Tests: `frontend/tests/autosave.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/autosave-forms.test.cjs`.
- **CI model (Riley, 2026-11-15): no automated tests before deployment; test afterward.** The existing required `offline-checks` performs Python syntax validation only (not a test pass), preserving branch protection without a settings change. Build, authentication, authorization and signing checks remain. Do not run automated tests before deployment, including locally. Independent source review precedes publication. The credential-free suite runs after a successful exact-revision Production deployment and on the 30-minute schedule; production smoke remains postdeploy. Never introduce writes to real user data for automatic tests. Existing live write tests require their separate explicit manual opt-in. See `docs/ci-cd.md`.
- **Type-check the frontend before you open a PR (Riley, 2026-11-15).** For any change under `frontend/`, run `npx tsc --noEmit` in `frontend/` (2–15 s; run `npm install` first if the worktree has no `node_modules`) and fix every error before opening the PR. This is the production build's own type check, not an automated test, so the rule above does not forbid it. No PR check runs tsc, so a type error otherwise surfaces only when the Vercel Production build fails after merge: the change is reverted and the merge train halts for every agent until the revert lands.
- **Logging surfaces (post 2026-05-31 cutover):** nutrition/food → **Cronometer** (just log in the app — the import is now automated via `cronometer_etl.py`, twice daily; the `cronometer_inbox/` + `cronometer_watcher.py` drop path is a manual fallback only); supplement pills → **Onyx** only (logged on Log's Supplements section, chat or an agent card; the library lives on `/supplements`; never Cronometer — avoids double-counting micros + preserves the UNII per-compound causal layer); meal timing → **automatic from Cronometer Gold per-entry timestamps** (`pds.meal_timing_from_cronometer`, COALESCE'd ahead of `meal_events` in the matrix). **The manual `/nutrition` meal-time quick-log + its read-only timing display were removed 2026-05-31** (user logs meals in real time in Cronometer instead); `pds.meal_events` is retained as the pre-Gold historical DB fallback in the COALESCE but has no UI entry surface anymore. Log meals close to when eaten so the auto-timing is truthful. **Post-midnight rule (both surfaces, 2026-06-09): log to the ACTUAL clock day/time, never manually backdate.** Cronometer: the Gold timestamp + −6h rule attribute a 1 AM pre-bed meal to yesterday automatically (servings + the `cronometer_nutrition_behavioral_daily` rollup); manually backdating would shift it a further day back. Supplements: logging pre-fills `intake_date` with `behavioral_today_now()` — don't "correct" the date to clock-today. A meal not in Cronometer can be logged from a plate photo (Log > Meal photo; see "Food by photo" under Database). `/nutrition` now shows: Cronometer macros, the Vitamins & Minerals panel, the "Today's Meals" list (Cronometer entries read-only, photo-logged meals with a remove ×, plus Uber Eats dishes and leftovers, read-only since 2026-11-15 (answered on Log), with a Today / Yesterday / day-before switch; see Delivery orders), and Body Weight (charts + Recent entries with delete; entered on Log). Read total micronutrient intake (food+supplement) from `pds.daily_micronutrient_totals`.
- **Agents chat (`/agents`, 2026-10-18)** is Riley's channel to the personal agents (morning briefing, approvals, questions, leads) and replaces WhatsApp. The backend is the sms-reminders Worker (`C:\Users\riley\sms-reminders`, routes `/onyx/*`): it verifies the Supabase access token against this project's JWKS (only Riley's user id), stores the thread in its D1 and sends Web Push itself, so Onyx holds no secret for it. Client: `src/lib/agents-api.ts`; UI (2026-10-22, Messages-style): `src/app/agents/AgentsApp.tsx` routes `/agents` to `AgentsInbox.tsx` (Unread = open cards with their buttons + unread agent messages, filter chips per agent; All = one conversation per agent) and `/agents?t=<agent>` to `AgentsChat.tsx` (one conversation; `#m<id>` jumps to a message, `&reply=A12` starts a card answer); shared bits in `agent-ui.tsx`. Full-screen; AppShell drops the mobile bars on this route; push: `public/sw.js` (excluded from middleware). iPhone push needs the home-screen app. **Clearing (2026-11-12):** Unread has **Clear all** (every open card cleared + every message marked read) and an **×** on each card, with an 8-second Undo; both call the Worker's `POST /onyx/dismiss`, which only hides cards from Unread and the badge (the agent's Notion row, the decision ledger and the card's buttons in its conversation are untouched; a card the agent shows again returns). While the inbox is on screen, and on every clear, the app also closes Onyx's notifications in the phone's notification list (`clearDeviceNotifications()` in `lib/agents-api.ts`, via `getNotifications()`). The phone bottom bar (2026-11-15, `components/MobileNav.tsx`, `lib/mobile-tabs.ts`) is by default Today · Agents (with the unread count from the cached inbox and `setBadge()`, no polling of its own) · Log (opens `/log`, the Log screen, accent, centre; the quick-log sheet is gone) · Chat (`/chat`, Onyx Chat, the health Q&A) · More (the drawer); the four slots around Log can be chosen in Personalize (phone drawer, see Personalize); a tab is named after the page it opens and Trends lives in More and on Today.
- **NYC apartment search (`/nyc`, 2026-11-07)** shows the nyc-scout agent's leads (personal-agents repo) as a Leaflet map (keyless OSM tiles darkened with a CSS filter — CARTO basemaps now require an API key) + sortable table. Data is NOT in Supabase: it lives in the Notion **"NYC Leads"** database (`967fdf52d6ee4aab9a5854298e564b43`, under Personal Agents; override with `NOTION_NYC_LEADS_DB`), read server-side with `NOTION_API_KEY` in `lib/nyc.ts` — so that database must stay shared with Onyx's Notion integration (a 404/403 renders as a "Couldn't load leads" banner and turns the `/status` NYC Scout card red). The scout upserts rows daily (key = `Number`); Riley's Interested / Toured / Pass buttons and note write ONLY the `Riley` / `Riley's note` properties (the note autosaves after a pause or when the box is left) via `PATCH /api/nyc/leads/[id]` (refuses pages outside the database, then `revalidateTag`), and the scout reads them next run. ISR hourly + silent mount revalidation via `GET /api/nyc/leads`, which answers from the Next data cache (tag `nyc-leads`, at most 5 minutes old, refreshed in the background; a live Notion query took 0.7-1.3 s, 2026-11-15). `/status` "NYC Scout" freshness = newest `Last verified` on a Live lead (daily-cadence thresholds).
- ETL scripts are standalone Python files at the project root (not in a package)
- Frontend follows Next.js App Router conventions (page.tsx per route)
- Supabase queries go in `frontend/src/lib/queries.ts`
- Reusable UI components go in `frontend/src/components/`
- **UI copy (2026-11-14).** Screens show headings, numbers, units, legends and labels. No explanatory paragraphs on the screen: how a number is worked out, where the data comes from, privacy and sync notes go behind `components/InfoHint.tsx` (an "i" popover, or `variant="disclosure"` for a longer "About this" row), closed by default; on a card use ChartCard / SectionHeader's `hint` prop. A subtitle is at most one short line. Keep visible: empty / failed / loading states (tests and the smoke suite match their exact text), error messages and anything that changes what Riley should do. **Exception, Riley's decision: statistical explanations stay visible** (what a test or estimate means, how to read it, assumptions, caveats, sample-size and "association, not causation" notes: `/analytics/hrv`, `/bland-altman`, the caffeine × HRV comparison, travel analysis, experiments); tighten them, never hide them, and use ChartCard's visible `info` prop for them. Put InfoHint next to a heading element, never inside it (its "About …" name would join the heading's name). Offline UI harnesses that copy only `lib/` files (procurement, financials CSV preview) use a plain `<details>` instead.
- **Navigation has a single source of truth: `frontend/src/lib/nav.ts` (`NAV_SECTIONS`).** `Sidebar.tsx` (desktop), `MobileNav.tsx` (mobile PWA drawer), and `CommandPalette.tsx` all consume it — add new routes there once (label, href, icon, shortcut, section) and every surface updates together. (Historical note: the two components used to maintain independent `nav` arrays that had to be edited in pairs; that convention is obsolete.)
- **Every integration must appear on `/status`.** When adding a new data source (ingestion ETL, enrichment subsystem, manually-driven log like supplements) it is **not done** until it has a card on the System Status page. The change touches three places, all in the same commit:
  1. `frontend/src/app/api/status/route.ts` — add a `MAX(...)` query for the source's data table to the `Promise.all` block, derive a `daysLag`, and append a new entry to the `sources` object. Use `deriveStatus(syncEntry, lag)` for ETL-driven sources, `deriveStatus(null, lag)` for manual-log sources (like habits / supplements where the data date itself is the freshness signal), or `enrichmentSource({label, entry})` for passive enrichment subsystems where "no new items today" is healthy. **Also add entries to the `CADENCE` and `METHOD` maps** — every source must declare its sync cadence and its integration method (`automated` / `semi-automated` / `manual`); semi-automated is reserved for cron imports that depend on a user-triggered export from the source app.
  2. `frontend/src/app/status/page.tsx` — add the source key to `SOURCE_ORDER`, `SOURCE_BADGE`, and `SOURCE_BADGE_COLOR`. If the source writes to `pds.sync_log`, also add it to `HISTORY_SOURCE_LABELS` (and any new `data_type` strings to `HISTORY_TYPE_LABELS`).
  3. If the source has an ETL, emit a `sync_log` heartbeat per run via `log_sync_entry()` (see `spotify_etl.py` for the pattern — Spotify writes three heartbeats per run: `spotify|plays`, `reccobeats|audio_features`, `musicbrainz|artist_tags`). The ETL should log `records_synced=0` rather than skipping the entry on no-op runs, so the status page sees a fresh heartbeat every cycle.
- SQL schema changes: create a .sql file, then apply via Supabase MCP or dashboard **Migrations sent through the Supabase MCP must contain no `DROP …` statement, not even `DROP TRIGGER/POLICY IF EXISTS` guards** — the connector asks for an interactive confirmation on anything destructive and returns "Migration was declined" when the session can't answer (2026-11-09: two declines until the guards were replaced with `CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER` + plain `CREATE POLICY`). It is not a Claude Code permission problem; no allow rule fixes it. The check appears to read the whole text, so strip header comments that mention the word too before sending.
- Always upsert (never raw insert) to keep ETL idempotent
- Never commit secrets (.env files are gitignored)
- **JSONB writes: pass dicts/lists directly to supabase-py; do NOT wrap with `json.dumps()`.** supabase-py serializes dicts to JSONB natively. Wrapping with `json.dumps()` produces a JSONB column whose top-level type is `'string'` rather than `'object'`/`'array'`, silently breaking every downstream JSON path query (`raw_json->>'field'` returns NULL). For dicts containing non-JSON-safe types (datetime, Decimal, numpy floats), use `json.loads(json.dumps(obj, default=str))` to sanitize-then-unwrap instead of double-encoding. Audit pass on 2026-05-26 found this bug at 25+ sites across every ETL and the analysis pipeline; backfilled via `(col #>> '{}')::jsonb` migration (`audit_jsonb_backfill_*`). The `pds.spotify_plays.raw_json` column was the only one that got it right from the start because the Spotify ETL was written after the bug pattern propagated elsewhere.
- **A bulk upsert must carry ONE key set (2026-11-13).** supabase-py sends a list upsert with `columns=` set to the union of every row's keys, and PostgREST writes NULL for a key a row does not have, overwriting the stored value. Never put rows that set an optional column next to rows that leave it out in the same `upsert([...])`; group them first (`journal_etl.uniform_batches`). Found live in `journal_etl.py`: unchanged entries carry no `embedding` key, so one edited page wiped the embedding of the 24 unchanged entries in its batch, the next hourly run re-embedded those and wiped the others, and semantic search was missing 1 or 24 of 45 entries at any moment. The other ETLs have not been checked for this.
- **Sync_log writers:** every ETL goes through `sync_log_helper.log_sync` (thin per-ETL wrappers are fine). Still hand-written: `garmin_etl.py` and `hrv_analysis.py:log_sync_entry`.
- **Every Python script that writes to Supabase emits a `pds.sync_log` heartbeat on EVERY run** — success AND failure, including uncaught exceptions. The audit caught silent retrain failures because `hrv_analysis.py` lacked a heartbeat, so its crashes were invisible to `/status`. Pattern: wrap `main()` in try/except in `if __name__ == "__main__"`, call `log_sync_entry(status='success'|'failed', records=N, error=str|None, duration=T)` in both branches. Without this, downstream effects (predictions, dashboards) keep looking healthy via cached state while the upstream pipeline is broken.
- **All date arithmetic in the HRV pipeline uses ET, never naive `date.today()`.** Both `hrv_predict.py` and `hrv_analysis.py` define `et_today()` (`datetime.now(ZoneInfo("America/New_York")).date()`) and use it for `prediction_date`, `MODEL_VERSION`, and every "today"/"tomorrow". A naive `date.today()` returns the **UTC** date, so a late-ET-evening CI run (already the next UTC day) mis-tags by +1. `hrv_analysis.py` had exactly this bug — its `_behavioral_v1` retrain rows got `prediction_date`/`model_version` = ET **+2** on evening runs (e.g. a forecast meant for tomorrow tagged day-after-tomorrow), which then dragged the `/status` freshness anchor into the future and showed every fresh source as "Degraded". Fixed 2026-06-06: ET-anchored `hrv_analysis.py`, deleted the 455 spurious rollover rows, and added a `pds.hrv_predictions_latest` tiebreak so the canonical `_v1` predict path always wins over `_behavioral_v1` for the same `(prediction_date, model, horizon_days)`. The `/status` freshness anchor (`spineMaxDate`) also no longer includes any prediction date — freshness must track observed data, never a forecast.
- **HRV alignment rule — one place: `hrv_alignment.py` (fixed 2026-11-10).** Row D of `pds.daily_health_matrix_behavioral` is behavioral day D, and its `whoop_hrv_rmssd` is the HRV of the sleep that CLOSES day D (WHOOP cycle dated by sleep onset − 6 h). Every consumer — Spearman / Welch / Granger / OLS, the causal layer, XGBoost, SARIMAX, Prophet and the backtest — scores a day-D behaviour against the SAME row's HRV via `al.add_outcome` / `al.outcome_series` (horizon h = the sleep closing day D+h−1). **Never build an outcome with `.shift(-…)` in the pipeline files** — the live test greps for it. Three column groups sit on another day in the matrix and are re-keyed by date in `al.realign_matrix` before any feature is derived (live checks 2026-11-10): WHOOP cycle totals (`whoop_day_strain`, `whoop_kilojoule`, `whoop_cycle_avg/max_hr`) describe the waking day AFTER row D's sleep (strain row D vs workouts row D+1 r=0.83, same row −0.01) → moved one day later; `eight_sleep_*` and `garmin_hrv_*` are keyed by the wake-up day (the previous night sits on row D; r=0.84 / 0.82 with WHOOP one row earlier) → moved one day earlier; the pandas merges of `garmin_sleep` / `garmin_hrv` / `eight_sleep_trends` extras go through `al.wake_date_to_closing_day`. `whoop_steps` (a WHOOP cycle total merged in pandas, not a matrix column) is dated from each cycle's own local start by `al.cycle_waking_day` (= cycle date + 1 for a night-time onset), cycles on one day summed. Garmin daily summary / stress / zones / training status stay on the clock day (activity = day D, overnight fields = the night before day D — both before row D's sleep). Garmin activities and WHOOP workouts are keyed on `onyx_behavioral_date`. Columns measured from the outcome sleep or anchored to its onset (`al.is_same_sleep`, `al.NEEDS_SLEEP_ONSET`) are mediators for the causal layer and never forecast inputs; other measurements of that night's HRV / RHR (`al.OUTCOME_TWINS`) are left out of the Spearman driver ranking. **The forecast** predicts from behavioral today's row (`al.serve_index`, ET − 6 h): tonight's sleep, the HRV Riley wakes up to tomorrow. `pds.hrv_predictions.prediction_date` = the behavioral date of the predicted sleep (same key as the matrix, the backtest and SARIMAX / Prophet; `actual_hrv` = matrix row `prediction_date`), and `/analytics/hrv` shows the wake-up date (+1). The model pickle carries `alignment = al.ALIGNMENT_VERSION`; `hrv_predict.py` refuses a pickle from another rule. It also carries `preprocessing` (2026-11-16, PIPE-07): the sparse Garmin columns (`HIGH_VALUE_SPARSE_FEATURES`) are forward-filled by ONE function, `fill_sparse_features` (at most 7 calendar days, on the full frame before rows without an outcome are dropped), in both `prepare_ml_data` and `serve_features`; `hrv_predict.py` serves a pickle stamped with the current fill with that same fill (an older, unstamped pickle keeps the old 7-row serving fill, which never matched its training, until the next retrain, with an `hrv_predict|preprocessing` partial heartbeat at most every 12 h) and refuses an unknown one (`GUARD model_preprocessing:`). Test: `tests/test_hrv_sparse_fill.py`. **Runtime guard** (`check_alignment` at the start of every retrain): a row whose HRV ≠ the recovery of the WHOOP cycle dated to it, or a re-keyed column that lines up with an adjacent row, fails the run (`GUARD hrv_alignment:` failed heartbeat); the alcohol canary (drinking must lower the same night's HRV more than the next night's) writes a `GUARD alcohol_canary:` partial heartbeat if it reverses. History: `b0828fa` (2026-04-04) introduced `shift(-1)`, correct under the old wake-day matrix; ADR-0001 (`165b879` + `a04d35e`, 2026-05-25) re-dated the matrix and left it, so from 2026-05-25 to 2026-11-10 every behaviour was scored against the night after the one it preceded (the re-audit's "DISPUTED P0", gemini F-001). Findings from that period are void; `pds.hrv_analysis_results_pre_alignment_20261110` holds the last pre-fix snapshot for comparison. Brief: `docs/handoff-hrv-alignment-2026-11-10.md`. **Dashboard side of the cutover (2026-11-11, `lib/hrv-forecast-cutover.ts`):** live rows in `pds.hrv_predictions_latest` with `prediction_date` < 2026-11-10 were made under the old one-night-late pairing; the walk-forward backtest rows are re-run by every retrain, so they follow the current rule. The Prediction-vs-Actual chart marks the first same-night forecast with a dashed line and a caption, draws no red miss-dots on old-pairing points, and the Residual Distribution histogram (`getHrvResiduals`, read from `hrv_predictions_eval` so rows carry `model_version`) leaves out nights whose only row is an old-pairing live forecast. It must NOT read `hrv_predictions_latest`: that view prefers a live row over a backtest row, so on ~170 dates it would serve the old-pairing live row and hide the same-night backtest residual the retrain rewrote (histogram 521 → 337 nights); `_eval` keeps the newest row, which is the backtest one (checked 2026-11-11: 514 backtest + 1 same-night live counted, 6 old-live-only nights left out). Any new frontend accuracy number built from forecast rows goes through `splitComparable` / `isSameNightComparable`.
- **Audit and review findings are settled against the data, never against docs or tests (2026-11-10).** A finding that claims wrong results may be closed as refuted only by a check on live data or by running the code. This file, the audit CONTEXT files and the existing tests are what such a finding disputes, so they are not evidence against it. Anything left DISPUTED or unverified when an audit or review ends becomes a roadmap row in the same session (Tasks DB, `Source = "Onyx Roadmap"`) at the audit's severity (P0 → `Critical`, P1 → `High`), with what would settle it in Notes. The weekly-review agent lists open Critical Onyx rows every week (personal-agents `agents/weekly-review.md`, step 3c). Origin: the one-night HRV misalignment above was flagged P0 on 2026-05-27, marked DISPUTED because it contradicted this file and `tests/test_phase2_alignment.py` (a synthetic frame that never reads the real matrix), never got a row, and ran for five and a half months. A pipeline whose results depend on an assumption about another layer (what a matrix row means, which day a date column describes) checks that assumption at run time and writes a `failed` or `partial` heartbeat whose `error_message` starts with `GUARD <name>:`; the devops agent matches that prefix and tells Riley instead of re-running.

### Smoke testing (Playwright)

Production tripwire for the class of bug `tsc` + `lint` cannot catch: a panel that **should** have data silently falling back to its empty state. The Spotify FK-ordering, HRV `JSON.parse`-of-jsonb, and supplements renamed-column regressions all shipped green and only surfaced when Riley clicked around production — each as an empty/broken panel.

- **Core principle:** *empty-state fallback present where data should exist = test failure.*
- **What it covers:** loads every dashboard page against **real production data** (authenticated) and asserts (a) the page isn't bounced to `/login` and doesn't hit the Next.js client error boundary, (b) a real data element rendered (recharts data marks / table rows / journal list items), and (c) **known empty-state fallback strings are ABSENT**. Two pages get precise assertions: `/analytics/hrv` (HRV Correlates draws bars + none of the "…not yet computed" / "Run hrv_analysis.py to compute correlations" fallbacks) and `/supplements` (the "Today's compound totals" table is populated whenever `/api/supplements/today` reports intakes — the test self-skips on a legitimately empty day rather than false-fail). Strings that can be legitimately empty (ReccoBeats-thin Spotify subsets, manual-entry meal/weight sections, range-windowed lists) are intentionally **not** asserted.
- **Files:** `frontend/playwright.config.ts` (single chromium project, `retries:1`, reuses a saved `storageState`), `frontend/e2e/auth.setup.ts` (logs in once via `/login`, saves `e2e/.auth/state.json` — gitignored), `frontend/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`. The `e2e/` dir + `playwright.config.ts` are excluded from `tsconfig.json` so they don't affect `next build`.
- **Run locally:** `cd frontend && npx playwright test` (add `--ui` to watch / debug). Defaults to the prod Vercel URL via `SMOKE_BASE_URL`.
- **Required env** (3 vars) — read from `frontend/.env.local` locally and from **GitHub repo secrets** in CI: `SMOKE_TEST_EMAIL`, `SMOKE_TEST_PASSWORD`, `SMOKE_BASE_URL`. (The login uses Supabase Auth email+password; use a real app account.)
- **Scheduled-workflow keepalive:** GitHub auto-disables every cron workflow after 60 days without a push (happened 2026-09-18 → 2026-10-03 outage). `.github/workflows/keepalive.yml` runs Mondays 12:00 UTC and re-enables every workflow via the Actions API, which resets that clock without a dummy commit. If Onyx goes quiet, `gh workflow list --all` and look for `disabled_inactivity` first.
- **CI:** `.github/workflows/smoke-test.yml` runs every 6h (`0 */6 * * *`) + `workflow_dispatch`. A failure = red X + GitHub's failure email (the alert); CI keeps no Playwright trace, screenshot or HTML report and uploads no artifact (the repo is public and those hold signed-in pages); reproduce a failure locally with `npx playwright test`, where traces and screenshots stay on. **Provenance (DEL-02, 2026-11-16):** a post-merge run counts for a commit only when the signed-in `/api/version` (commit + `VERCEL_URL`) shows that commit and deployment before and after the tests (`e2e/provenance.ts`); anything else is `deferred` (pending verification), never a pass, and post-merge reverts only a commit that failed twice on its own deployment while its parent's `post-merge/smoke` commit status passed in the 3 h before that failure, and a revert or halt is dropped only by an exact smoke pass whose run started after the failing attempt ended (attempt times from the smoke runs themselves, kept in each receipt, never when a receipt was written). `post-merge/smoke` is written only while the repository variable `SMOKE_RECEIPT_STATUS` is `on` (until then every smoke failure halts, never reverts); once written it is part of master's combined commit status, so the deploy result is the `Vercel` context, never the combined `state`. The 6-hourly run tests the newest deployed master commit, matched on the commit, and goes red when it could not confirm what production serves (rules in `docs/ci-cd.md`).
- **Adding a page:** append to the `PAGES` array (or add a dedicated `test.describe`) in `smoke.spec.ts`. For a manual-entry page whose own in-window empty-state is legitimate (e.g. `/caffeine` after a logging pause), set `legitimateEmpty` to that exact string — the test then SKIPs with a reason instead of failing; `/caffeine` additionally honours `?days=N` (7–730; the ISR seed is discarded for non-default windows) and a dedicated test loads `/caffeine?days=720` to assert the intake chart draws from real historical rows; note the shared Sidebar sparkline is a recharts svg, so the generic "charts" probe alone cannot tell "page empty" from "chart broke". Prefer text-absence of the real empty-state string + a "some data element exists" check over brittle per-element selectors.

## Backlog & Open Items

**Canonical backlog: the unified [Tasks database (Notion)](https://www.notion.so/98725d38095c402585a8cef80980e946) filtered to `Source = "Onyx Roadmap"`** — data source ID `6149d32a-66be-4e54-ab1b-28c4a11c6543`. The Onyx backlog now lives here alongside other personal task sources; **always filter by `Source = "Onyx Roadmap"`** when reading or writing Onyx items so you don't touch unrelated tasks. Schema: Task / Status (Not Started / In Progress / Blocked / Done) / Priority (Critical / High / Medium / Low) / Component (Database, ETL, Analysis, Visualization, AI/MCP, Security) / Phase (1-4) / Source / Notes. Useful views: "Active" (Status ≠ Done, sorted by Priority) and "By Source".

> History: this backlog was the standalone **Implementation Roadmap** DB (`bb09c504…` / data source `6041b9d9…`) until 2026-06-14, when it was merged into the unified Tasks DB and archived to Notion trash. Don't write to the old IDs — they point at the trashed database. Value remaps from the old schema: Priority `P0→Critical`, `P1→High`, `P2→Medium`, `P3 - Nice to have→Low`; Status `To Do→Not Started`.

**This is the source of truth for "what's open" and "what's next" on Onyx** — read it filtered to `Source = "Onyx Roadmap"` and `Status ≠ Done`. Do not track open items in this file — AGENTS.md is for *shipped* capability + conventions; the roadmap is for *pending* work. When shipping a feature, mark the matching task `Done` (or create-then-Done one in the same call) and refresh its Notes with the commit / module / route. When noticing a bug or polish opportunity you won't fix in the current session, create a `Not Started` page (tagged `Source = "Onyx Roadmap"`) with enough context that a future session can pick it up cold.

Mechanics: use the Notion MCP — `notion-update-page` (`command: "update_properties"`, `properties: {"Status": "Done", "Notes": "..."}`) for existing pages; `notion-create-pages` (`parent: {"type":"data_source_id", "data_source_id":"6149d32a-66be-4e54-ab1b-28c4a11c6543"}`) for new ones. Always set `Source: "Onyx Roadmap"` plus Component / Phase / Priority / Status (and Notes) — leaving any blank makes the entry hard to triage.

**Notion MCP read quota (2026-11-11).** `notion-query-data-sources` in SQL or rows mode shares one workspace-wide allowance across every agent session; when it runs out it fails with `usage_limit_reached` for up to an hour (2026-11-11: hit during the post-merge verification). Do not retry it. Read the backlog through the saved view **"Onyx Roadmap (open)"** (Source = Onyx Roadmap, Status ≠ Done) in view mode: `notion-query-data-sources` with `{"mode":"view","view_url":"https://www.notion.so/98725d38095c402585a8cef80980e946?v=416bf5b44bf28159ab40000cf40dff49"}` (paginate with `next_cursor`), which stays available when the allowance is used up, and use `notion-fetch` by URL for a known row. `notion-update-page` and `notion-create-pages` are not limited. A `Notes` property update replaces the whole value, so re-read the row right before appending (other sessions write to the same rows). **Preferred for agents with shell access: `python tools/notion_tasks.py`** (`list [--all]`, `notes <id|url>`, `append <id|url> "text"`, `status <id|url> "Done"`) uses the Notion REST API with the "Personal agents backup" integration (token in `~/.notion_token`; the Tasks DB is shared with it) and is not covered by the MCP allowance; `append` keeps the existing Notes text, links and formatting exactly, so there is nothing to retype.

## Personalize (2026-11-15)

Business Central-style page personalization (`components/personalize/*`, `lib/personalize-*.ts`). **Personalize** in the sidebar footer, the phone's More drawer and the command palette (on every page) opens the "Personalizing <page>" banner: **Done**, **Clear personalization** (this page, with a confirm), **Cancel** (drops this session's changes), **+ Add part** (hidden parts). Every part gets a frame with a drag handle (mouse: drag anywhere on the part; touch: the handle, plus up / down buttons on phones) and a menu: Move up / down / to top, Width (full / half / third, desktop only), Collapse by default, Hide. While a part is dragged (or moved with the keyboard: Space on the handle, arrow keys, Space to drop, Escape to cancel) an accent line shows exactly where it lands and the parts on either side move apart. Hidden parts show as hatched slots with Show while personalizing and are not rendered otherwise. Tables built with `PersonalTable` get a Columns chooser (show / add hidden columns, reorder) and a menu per header (move, hide, freeze pane); used on `/financials` transactions and `/status` Sync History. Leaving the page while personalizing keeps the changes. Keyboard (2026-11-16, `components/personalize/banner-focus.ts`): the banner is drawn in a host prepended to `<body>`, so it is first in the Tab order, takes focus when it opens if focus was lost (an opener that stays enabled, Today's overview Personalize, keeps it, and so does an open modal, the phone drawer of a side-panel session) and gives it back when it closes to the control that opened it, else the More tab, else (started from the command palette) the shown Search control (`data-palette-trigger`), but never to a control behind a modal that is still open (Done / Cancel in the phone drawer's arranger: focus stays in the drawer); `html` scroll padding in `globals.css` keeps a focused control clear of the phone header and tab bar, the banner and the check-in save bar, and the side panel starts with a "Skip to page content" link to the title of the page in view (the wordmark is not a heading).
- **Side panel and scroll order (`layout.areas`, `lib/nav-areas.ts`, `lib/nav-arrange.ts`, `components/personalize/NavArranger.tsx`):** while Personalize is on (any page; on a page without parts Personalize opens just this, "Personalizing the side panel"), the desktop sidebar's pages can be dragged within their section and sections by their name, with the same accent line; the phone drawer shows up / down buttons (44 px) plus Done / Cancel. Plain clicks do not navigate while arranging (Ctrl / Cmd + click does). The result is the app's ONE area order, `grids.main.order` of the UI preference `layout.areas` (area ids = `areaId(href)`: `/analytics/hrv` -> `analytics.hrv`): the sidebar and the drawer order pages within sections by it and sections by their first page, and the continuous scroll (`lib/feed-order.ts`) follows it. Saved with the page's changes on Done (`personalize-store` keeps a draft per key). **The scroll follows the draft live (2026-11-15):** each change to the `layout.areas` draft fires `LAYOUT_DRAFT_EVENT` (`onyx:layout-draft`, detail `{key, layout}`) on window, and `layout: null` when the session ends (after Done's save, or on Cancel / Clear); `ContinuousScroll` uses the draft while it is on and the saved copy otherwise, without importing the store (shell bundle); a scroll that starts listening mid-session fires `LAYOUT_DRAFT_REQUEST_EVENT` and an open session answers with its draft. This device's copy wins over the server's whenever it exists, even when it is a cleared (empty) layout, so Clear never brings the server's old order back. When the order changes under the scroll, `keepInOrder()` keeps the pages around the routed one that still follow each other in the new order and drops the rest, and the scroll grows again from there (it used to start again from the routed page). A full page load paints the saved order before React: `Sidebar` renders `navBootScript()` (plain ES5, reads `onyx.layout.areas`, writes CSS `order` on `[data-nav-area]` / `[data-nav-section]`), removed once React renders the same order; the drawer is closed at load, so it just re-renders. Keep `data-nav-area` / `data-nav-section` and the flex containers when editing the sidebar. Today's overview cards are a `layout.areas` grid too (`components/overview/Overview.tsx`: `<PartGrid storeKey={AREAS_KEY} target={false} bare>`, part ids = area ids, "Hide (also from the scroll)"), so the cards, the side panel and the scroll share one order; the area grid is editable in every Personalize session. The old `overview.cards` value is copied into `layout.areas` the first time the server has no area order (`personalize-store` `migrateOverviewCards`), then ignored; the overview's own Edit mode is gone. Tests: `frontend/tests/nav-areas.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/personalize-draft.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/feed-order.test.cjs`.
- **Pin, hide and the bottom bar (2026-11-15, same `layout.areas` value, read by `areaPrefsOf()` / `arrangeNav()` in `lib/nav-areas.ts`, written by `lib/nav-arrange.ts`):** while personalizing, each page in the side panel has a Pin button (to the **Quick access** group; drag a page into Quick access to pin it there, drag within it to reorder) and a ⋯ menu with Move up / down, **Hide from navigation** and **Hide from scroll** (two separate toggles). Hidden pages are listed under **Show hidden** (also outside Personalize, so they stay one click away; the command palette and G shortcuts still reach them). Stored as grid entries: `grids.main.hide` = left out of the continuous scroll (and its Today card, as before), `grids.quick` = Quick access (`order` = pinned pages in order, `hide[id]` true = a default pin, `QUICK_ACCESS`, taken out, false = another page pinned; the default pins store nothing), `grids.nav.hide` = left out of the sidebar and the drawer (hiding also unpins), `grids.tabs.order` = the phone bar's four slots. **Quick access pages lead the continuous scroll** (`parseAreaOrder` returns `pinned`, `areaOrder` puts them first; `DEFAULT_PINNED` in `lib/feed-order.ts` must equal `QUICK_ACCESS`). The phone drawer shows Quick access first too, and in Personalize (drawer, up to 340 px wide) a **Bottom bar** box: a preview plus one select per slot (Left 1, Left 2, Log fixed in the centre, Right 1, Right 2), each a page or More; choosing a page already on the bar swaps the two (`chooseTab`, `tabSlots` in `lib/mobile-tabs.ts`); with More off the bar the drawer opens from a menu button in the top bar. Pre-paint: the sidebar renders EVERY page twice, once in Quick access (`data-nav-quick`) and once in its section (`data-nav-area`), the copy not shown carrying `data-off="true"` and `hidden`, so `navBootScript()` can show a saved panel before React with CSS only (tests and the perf spec count only links without `data-off`); the bottom bar's `tabsBootScript()` keeps changed slots invisible (never moved) until React renders them. Tests: `frontend/tests/nav-areas.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/mobile-tabs.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/feed-order.test.cjs`.
- **How to make a page personalizable (the exact API; convert pages in parallel with this):**
  1. `import { Part, PartGroup, PartGrid } from "@/components/personalize/PartGrid";` (client component; a server component may render it).
  2. Right after the page header (keep the header, its `data-testid="page-heading"` and any range filter OUTSIDE the grid): `<PartGrid page="/your/route" title="Page title">`.
  3. Each section becomes `<PartGroup id="section-id" title="Section name" header={<the section heading element/>}>` and EVERY card, KPI tile, chart, panel and list block in it becomes `<Part id="part-id" title="Readable name" width="…">` as a direct child (no wrapper divs between them; drop the old grid wrapper and the `mb-*` margins: the group's grid spaces parts). Widths: `full` (default), `half` (2 per row from 1024 px), `third` (3 from 1024, 2 from 768), `quarter` (KPI tiles: 4 from 1024, 2 below). A part can also sit at the top level (a group of one). Parts move within and between the groups of the page; groups move, hide and collapse as one.
  4. Ids: lower-case, stable, unique on the page, never reused for something else (they are the saved layout's keys); titles are what Personalize shows. `{cond && <Part/>}` is fine (a data-dependent part keeps its saved place).
  5. Keep each chart's `<DeferredMount minHeight>` INSIDE its `<Part>`, keep data fetching where it is (a part that fetches its own data stops fetching when hidden), keep `isAnimationActive={false}` and every smoke-test string.
  6. Tables: build them with `PersonalTable` (`components/personalize/PersonalTable.tsx`: `columns` with `{id, label, th, td, hidden?, locked?}` and `rows` of `{key, className?, cells: {colId: node}}`) inside a `<Part>`; Personalize then offers the column chooser and the freeze pane.
  7. Where the page renders: client-rendered pages (the ISR dashboards with a `dynamic(ssr:false)` client) need nothing more. A force-dynamic server page passes `parts={[{id, title, node, members?}]}` (plain data) and `initial={layoutFromCookie(cookie)}` and is added to `SSR_LAYOUT_PAGES` (see `/financials`). A static server-rendered page (hydrates, like Today) uses `<PartGrid mode="css">` per container: order and hide only, no moves between containers. A page drawn inside the continuous scroll needs no change (`useInFeed` keeps the routed page as the Personalize target).
  8. Run `npx tsc --noEmit`; check it on production after the deploy (Personalize, drag one part into another group, Cancel).
- **Opting in (details):** wrap the page's blocks in `<PartGrid page="/route" title="…">` with `<Part id title width?>` as DIRECT children (`{cond && <Part/>}` is fine); ids are stable, lower-case, never reused for something else. Keep each part's `DeferredMount` (and its `minHeight`) inside the part, keep data fetching where it was, keep `data-testid="page-heading"` and the header outside the grid. A block holding its own `<PartGrid zone="…">` is a `<Part nested>` (parts then move only within that zone; prefer a `<PartGroup>`, whose parts also move between sections: `/status` source cards are a group with `gridClassName="pz-grid pz-tiles …"`, the dense tile columns, where Full / Half still widen a card). A server-rendered (force-dynamic) page passes `parts={[{id, title, node}]}` plus `initial={layoutFromCookie(cookie)}` and is listed in `SSR_LAYOUT_PAGES`. Adopted so far: `/analytics/hrv` (sections; its "On this page" list follows the layout via `usePartOrder`), `/sleep`, `/nutrition`, `/activities`, `/financials`, `/status`, `/today` (Today's own panels, 2026-11-15: ONE `<PartGrid page="/today" mode="css">` in `TodayClient.tsx` holding every panel AND the three section headings ("Heading: What needs you" etc.) as parts, so a panel moves into another section by dragging it past that section's heading; the header (h1 + Refresh) stays fixed and the overview cards keep their own `layout.areas` grid below; CSS mode = move and hide only, no width or collapse; the jump links do not follow the layout).
- **Storage:** one overlay per page in `pds.ui_preferences` under `layoutKey(route)` (`layout.analytics.hrv`, `layout.sleep`, …; at most 40 characters, the live table's limit), value `{v: 1, grids: {main: {order, hide, width, collapse}, <group id>: {order}}, tables: {<id>: {order, hide, freeze}}}` (top-level order in `main`, each group's order in its own entry, so a part listed in another group's order has moved there; hide / width / collapse are page-wide in `main`) holding only differences from the default: new parts appear at their default spot, removed parts drop out, Clear stores `{v: 1}`. This device keeps it in localStorage `onyx.layout.*` (wiped on sign-out); server-rendered pages also in a cookie scoped to the page's path. `?personalize=off` shows default layouts for one load.
- **Performance rules (Riley: "we need to be really fast"):** (1) the first render reads localStorage synchronously, so the personalized layout is the first thing painted: no request before paint, no re-render, nothing moves (the ISR dashboards render their parts client-side with `ssr:false`; server-rendered pages get it from the cookie); (2) a hidden part is not rendered, so it mounts nothing and fetches nothing (Weather and PM2.5 on `/analytics/hrv` fetch only when shown); page-level data (the ISR payload, `/api/status`) is shared and still loaded; (3) everything for editing (`PersonalizeEditor.tsx`: banner, frames, drag, menus, column chooser) is a separate chunk loaded only when Personalize starts (or on hover / focus of the entry); normal pages carry only `PartGrid` + `personalize-layout` + the store, and the shared shell only the tiny `personalize-registry`; (4) the server copy is checked in an idle slot after load, at most every 15 minutes per page, through the shared client cache; a newer copy from another device applies the next time the page opens (Personalize adopts it before editing); a change is written only when the layout changed (Done / Clear), in the background, retried on the next open when offline; (5) dragging writes the indicator and the gap straight to the DOM (no React render per pointer move). Tests: `frontend/tests/personalize.test.cjs` (overlay, columns, cookie, drop position).

## Log screen (2026-11-15)

`/log` (nav "Log", G 1, Quick access with Today, the phone bar's centre Log tab) is where Riley makes every daily entry on one screen (Riley: "make it really easy for me to do all my entries from one screen"). Frame: `app/log/page.tsx` (static shell, title "Log") → `LogLoader.tsx` (`dynamic(ssr:false)`, skeleton `LogSkeleton.tsx` painted by the server) → `LogClient.tsx`: the h1 "Log", a Meal photo link (`/nutrition?log=photo`, what the old quick-log sheet offered besides the pages below) and ONE `<PartGrid page="/log">` whose parts are the sections, in this default order: `say-it`, `check-in` (nested), `journal`, `supplements`, `uber-eats`, `weight` (half), `reading` (half). **One file per section** in `app/log/sections/` (`SayIt`, `CheckIn`, `Journal`, `Supplements`, `UberEats`, `Weight`, `Reading`), each bundled with the page's own first scripts (LogLoader imports `LogClient`, which imports every section, so the HTML loads all of the screen's code in parallel with the framework, with no chunk round trip after hydration; each keeps its `dynamic()` wrapper and skeleton; a hidden part is never mounted; their first reads start with the HTML, see the speed-budget bullet), loading with `SectionSkeleton` from `sections/ui.tsx` (also `LogCard`, `DraftMark`, `LogSaveButton`), reading its data from the client cache first and writing ONLY through the existing routes with their existing validation and date rules, ONLY after Riley's own input or his Say it words (autosave, `lib/autosave.ts`: no Save buttons; never on open, never on scroll). The Check-in section is the real `CheckInClient` (`feed embedded`, inside `<InFeed>` so its own grid never claims Personalize; its own autosave, drafts and rules), not a copy; `omitParts` leaves out its "say-it-once" / "journal-draft" / "uber-eats" / "uber-eats-leftovers" parts once `REPLACES_CHECKIN_SAY_IT_ONCE` (SayIt.tsx) / `REPLACES_CHECKIN_JOURNAL_DRAFT` (Journal.tsx, true) / `REPLACES_CHECKIN_UBER_EATS` (UberEats.tsx) are true; without its journal card, the journal draft of the check-in's own Say it once goes to the Journal section as a `journal` draft (words handed over by the Log Say it box get none from the check-in: that box drafts its own). `/check-in` stays its own page for the 21:30 card's deep links, but has no FeedSection: the scroll shows the check-in once, inside Log. **Drafts (`lib/log-drafts.ts`):** the Say it box writes suggestions with `setLogDrafts({...}, said)`; each section reads `useLogDrafts(section)` (`checkin` = `{text}`, run through the check-in's own read-only voice fill; `supplements` = product or stack + amount + times + date; `ubereats` = portion or leftover per `item_id`; `weight` = lb + date; `reading` = book + minutes + chapters + medium + recall + date; `journal` = body VERBATIM + source + optional title / mood / confidence / topics + `asked`; exact types in the file), shows them marked "from what you said" and calls `clear(draft.id)` after its Save succeeded or on dismiss. Memory only: no storage, no request, gone on reload; a draft never overwrites what Riley tapped. Warm-up: `/log` in `PINNED`, `DATA` and `CODE` of `AppData.tsx` (a section adds its GET reads to `DATA["/log"]`). Tests: `frontend/tests/log-drafts.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/mobile-nav-accessibility.test.cjs`, smoke `/log`.
- **Log: Weight and Reading (2026-11-15, `app/log/sections/Weight.tsx`, `Reading.tsx`).** Weight shows today's row from `GET /api/weight?days=365` (a year, so the last weigh-in still shows after a long gap) with its source (Tanita / Manual), else the latest; one pounds field (16 px, no iOS zoom) + Day (ET calendar day, max today) that **save themselves** (`lib/weight-entry.ts`, 2026-11-15) through `POST /api/weight` with the /nutrition rules (0 to 1000 lb; a day that has a row is replaced and becomes manual, the line under the field says so): 1 s after the last keystroke (4 s when the number is over 30 % from the last weigh-in, a half-typed "18"), at once on Enter, leaving the field or hiding the page; later edits replace the same day; moving the Day after a save moves the entry (the earlier day gets back what it had: a manual row its old value and note, a Tanita or empty day is cleared and the next scale run restores Tanita; a day older than the rows on screen is read first); Undo does the same; an emptied field saves nothing. Reading shows the current book from `GET /api/reading` (same key as Today and /reading): chapters, percent, pace, streak, Kindle position, and that behavioral day's sessions read-only (Kindle ones are the harvester's); below them an Audio / Paper / Other session (from / through chapter with next-chapter chips, minutes, day, recall note stored verbatim) that saves itself: created through `POST /api/reading/sessions` once "Through" is set and Riley pauses or leaves a field, then every edit updates it through `PATCH /api/reading/sessions/[id]`, with "New session" and "Remove this session" (a Say it draft that arrives after a session was saved starts a new one only once the old one is saved; otherwise its words wait, with Use); the day is sent only when chosen, so the server's behavioral today applies. A draft that arrives after a session was saved starts a new one. Both apply their Say it draft to fields Riley has not changed (marked "from what you said", Use / Dismiss when it could not be applied), save it like his own entry and clear it after the save.
- **Log: Journal (2026-11-15, `app/log/sections/Journal.tsx`).** Today's entries already in Onyx (`GET /api/journal/list` for the New York date, `journalTodayUrl` in `lib/journal-file.ts`; titles + mood, each linking to its Notion page; memory-only in the client cache, like every `/api/journal` key), a box for a typed or dictated entry ("Captured by" voice / typed: voice when a dictation inserts a whole phrase at once, typed otherwise, until Riley picks one) that **saves by itself (2026-11-15, no Save button)**: 5 s after typing stops, on leaving the box or when the page is hidden, `POST /api/journal/file` files ONE entry (see the journal bullet under Database: body verbatim, Date today in New York, labels Riley did not set inferred in one model call), then further edits update THAT page through **`PATCH /api/journal/file/[pageId]`** (3 s pause; only the changed paragraphs; Source always; labels re-inferred only when the entry changed by a quarter of its words and 8+ words (`shouldRelabel`), and written only where the page still holds the label this box wrote last). The route is session-only, never in `isCiRoute()`, same-origin JSON with the 32 KB cap, refuses unknown fields, never changes the date, and refuses pages outside the Entries database or in the trash (410 `gone`). Before every send the box writes that version's SHA-256 (a synchronous implementation, so a send from a closing tab is covered), its paragraph hashes and the entry's day into the device copy. Requests carry `known_sha` (the versions filed or sent) and, for the PATCH, `known_paras`. A page holding one of those versions, or only those paragraphs (an update Notion stopped halfway, which the retry finishes), is this box's own; anything else was edited in Notion and is not overwritten (409 `changed`: Riley picks Replace, which sends `force`, or File as a new entry). A restored entry files on its own day, and POST looks for a page of that day by hash with no 15-minute limit (`stale` → the box PATCHes it), so a lost answer never leaves a second entry. "New entry" saves what the box holds first (restored words included) and clears nothing unless that save went through; only Discard drops restored words. Entries filed here are listed under Today at once and reach Journal after the hourly sync. Words not saved yet are kept on the device (`onyx.journal.unsaved`, wiped on sign-out, removed once saved; it holds the words, the entry's day and hashes, never another version's text) and restored after a reload or a closed tab, waiting for Keep or an edit (nothing is written on open or on scroll). A `journal` draft fills an empty box (its labels are sent only while the words are unchanged): a "journal:" note (`asked: true`, set by `sayItJournalDraft`) is filed by itself; a reflection the model picked out, or the check-in's Say it once draft, waits for Keep (or an edit) and is never stored on the device. Over words or a filed entry it is only offered (Add to entry / New entry with these words / Dismiss). For the Say it box, `lib/journal-file.ts` has `sayItJournalDraft(text, modelJournal)` with `SAY_IT_JOURNAL_SCHEMA` / `SAY_IT_JOURNAL_RULES`: a "journal:" prefix makes the rest the entry, otherwise the model's `is_journal` decides and its `excerpt` is used only when it is an exact quote of the note (else the whole note). Tests: `frontend/tests/journal-file.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/log-drafts.test.cjs`.

**Uber Eats section (`app/log/sections/UberEats.tsx`, 2026-11-15).** The same dishes and controls as `/check-in` (`components/DeliveryPortions.tsx`: the day's delivered restaurant dishes plus the 2 days before still unanswered, "Ate: All / 3/4 / 1/2 / 1/4 / None", leftovers with the time eaten), with a Today / Yesterday / day-before switch (the embedded check-in's day tabs no longer show Uber Eats: `REPLACES_CHECKIN_UBER_EATS = true`, and `CheckInClient` skips its dish read when both parts are omitted; `/check-in` itself is unchanged). Taps save on their own exactly as on `/check-in` (`/nutrition` shows the same lists read-only: `DeliveryDishList` / `LeftoverList` without `onPick`) (PATCH `/api/delivery/items/[id]`, PUT `.../leftovers`, `createPortionSaver`). The line under the heading, "Uber Eats checked N min ago", is the newest `ubereats|items` heartbeat (`getUberEatsCheck`, time + status only), read every minute while the screen is visible; when it moves, the dishes are read again (dishes already on screen stay); over 25 min it says the PC looks off or asleep, a failed run is red with a Status link. Say it drafts (`useLogDrafts("ubereats")`) are drawn dashed "from what you said" on the matching control, listed in a box with Dismiss and stored on their own 0.8 s later (each suggestion tried once; one still waiting gets Retry): portions first, then leftovers (a leftover needs its delivery-day portion below All); a dish Riley tapped since the section loaded keeps his answer; a draft for another day offers "Show <day>" (and switches to it when the draft has nothing for the day on screen and Riley has not picked a day). Speed: `useDeliveryDishes` takes an opt-in `cacheKey` (`deliveryWindowKey`, under `delivery:` so every Uber Eats write drops it and the registered fetcher reads it again) and paints the cached window on the first render; `getDeliveryWindow` reads the leftover rows in parallel with the dishes (by eaten day, one round trip fewer, for every caller); `DATA["/log"]` warms behavioral today, today's window and the heartbeat. Pure parts in `lib/log-ubereats.ts`.

**Say it box (2026-11-15, `sections/SayIt.tsx`, `lib/log-voice.ts`).** Riley types or dictates (the keyboard's microphone) and taps Fill: the words go at once to the embedded check-in as `{checkin: {text}}` (its own read-only fill: daytime answers and last night's sleep, no journal draft for these words; `REPLACES_CHECKIN_SAY_IT_ONCE` is true, so the check-in's own Say it once box is left out on /log) and, in parallel, to **`POST /api/log/voice`** (`{text, date?}`, date = behavioral today by default, the check-in's 3-day window; owner session only, never in `isCiRoute()`; same-origin JSON, 16 KB cap, unknown fields refused; ONE `claude-sonnet-5-5` call, effort low, a static JSON schema so it compiles once; writes nothing, never stores or logs the text). It answers drafts in the shared shapes: journal (`sayItJournalDraft` with `SAY_IT_JOURNAL_SCHEMA` / `SAY_IT_JOURNAL_RULES` from `lib/journal-file.ts`, the model also gets `known_topics`: a "journal:" prefix, or a note the model calls a reflection, gives an entry of the words verbatim with suggested labels); supplements (a product id from the active list, else the chat tool's `resolveProduct` on the said name; amount + unit; "HH:MM" times; date) and stacks by id; Uber Eats portions (the day's dishes and the 2 days before whose portion is unset, the check-in's rule) and leftovers for that day (`leftoverQuestions`, or a dish whose portion the same note sets below All; portions come first in the list; a leftover time still to come is dropped, the guard would refuse it); weight in lb (kg converted, 60 to 600); one reading session (a book being read; the recall is kept only when it is found word for word in the text, `findVerbatim`, never a paraphrase). Plus one readable line per draft (`summary`, shown under the box per section) and `not_matched` (things said that match no list). The model sees only the words and the candidate lists: product and stack names, the last 4 days' dish names with restaurant and portion state, the books being read with chapter counts. A list that fails to load is named in `unavailable` and that section is not filled. An empty section gets no draft (its earlier suggestion stays). The words and the last result live in module memory only (kept while scrolling, gone on reload).

**One screen, joined up (2026-11-15, `app/log/jump.ts`).** Each Log part starts with a zero-height anchor `log-part-<part id>` (`logAnchorId`, `logHref`): `/log#log-part-supplements` opens the screen at that section and keeps it at the top while the sections above grow from their skeletons, until Riley scrolls, touches or types or 2.5 s pass (`jumpToLogPart(part, {settle})`; only the routed page reads the hash, never a copy in the scroll). Today's "Start your check-in", its quick links (Supplement, Habit = the check-in, Weight) and "Open check-in" open these, and the desktop jump list has Log instead of Check-in; `/check-in` stays its own page for the 21:30 card's dated deep links. Under the Say it box, every section a Fill gave something is a row that scrolls to it (a section hidden in Personalize says so instead), and the check-in's row is live: the embedded check-in reports its own fill (`reportCheckinFill` / `useCheckinFill` in `lib/log-drafts.ts`: waiting, the "Filled N answers" line, or why nothing was filled), because its own Say it once box, where that line used to appear, is left out on /log. Embedded, the check-in drops its `max-w-xl` column so it lines up with the other sections. On a touch screen every field on /log reads at 16 px (`.log-screen` rule in `globals.css`), so iOS never zooms the page in on focus, including the shared check-in and Uber Eats controls.

## One place to enter data (2026-11-15)

Riley: "reduce duplicate pages where I can input data." Every daily entry is made on `/log` (check-in and habits, journal, supplements, Uber Eats portions and leftovers, weight, reading sessions). The domain pages keep their charts and history, the management Log cannot do, and corrections, and show a `components/LogLink.tsx` link (`logHref(part)` from `app/log/jump.ts`) where their form used to be:
- `/habits`: no "Today's Habits" checklist or backdate picker ("Answer today's habits on Log" in the header). The heatmap's Yes / No / Clear popover stays for corrections (`/api/habits/complete`, same guards). Habits are still managed in Notion.
- `/supplements`: no tap-to-log on products or stacks and no Intake date / time override; `lib/supplement-writes.ts` (the Saving / Not saved receipts of those taps) went with them. Kept: the library (add, scan, custom product, remove from the list), the stack editor, Today's intakes and Recent intakes with edit / delete (a refused delete stays listed with Retry), compound totals. The add-product step may still log the first dose of a NEW product (Log cannot add products).
- `/nutrition`: Uber Eats dishes and leftovers are read-only (`DeliveryDishList` / `LeftoverList` without `onPick` draw the answer as text: "Ate 3/4", "Ate All (assumed)", "Not answered (counts as thrown out)"); no weight form (Recent entries keeps delete; `/nutrition?log=weight` forwards to `/log#log-part-weight`). The meal photo logger stays here (Log's Meal photo opens it).
- `/reading`: no "Log a session" form; Review, highlights (add, Kindle import, edit) and the Session log stay.
- Unchanged: `/check-in` (its own page for the 21:30 card's dated deep links, not in the scroll), the chat write tools and agent cards (other ways to say the same thing, not pages).

A new kind of daily entry goes into a Log section, not onto a domain page. Part ids of the removed forms (`weight-log`, `log-session`, `log-intake`) are kept for the link or the list that replaced them, so saved layouts keep their place. Tests: `frontend/tests/supplements-page.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/delivery-leftovers.test.cjs` (read-only lists), `frontend/tests/checkin.test.cjs`.

## Desktop app (2026-11-14)

Onyx on Windows is the site installed as a PWA from Edge or Chrome (own window, Start menu + taskbar entry, Windows notifications for `/agents` via the existing `public/sw.js`, taskbar badge via `setAppBadge`); no Electron / Tauri shell, nothing to sign or update. `public/manifest.json`: **`id` and `start_url` stay `"/"`** (Edge derives the app identity from them; changing either turns existing installs into a second app), `scope` `/`, `theme_color` / `background_color` = `--color-surface` (`#1A1A18`, the title bar), `launch_handler` `focus-existing` (a launch focuses the open window; `components/DesktopApp.tsx:DesktopLaunchHandler` reads `launchQueue` and moves it only for a jump-list shortcut, a plain launch keeps the page that was open; rule in `lib/desktop-app.ts:launchTarget`), and a taskbar jump list (`shortcuts`: Today, Agents, Log, Status; every URL must be a `NAV_SECTIONS` route other than `/`). Inside the installed window (display mode `standalone` or `window-controls-overlay`, which have no browser toolbar) the sidebar shows **Reload** (`ReloadAppButton`, same as Ctrl R / F5; hidden in a normal tab and in `minimal-ui`). The sidebar shows **Install desktop app** only when the browser offers the install (`beforeinstallprompt`, caught at module load); Edge's address-bar install icon does the same. Start-at-sign-in is Edge's own per-app setting (`edge://apps`). The command-palette hint reads Ctrl K on Windows. **Go-to shortcuts (2026-11-15):** press G, then a page's key within 1.5 s to open it client-side on the warmed page (`lib/go-shortcuts.ts`, listener in `components/CommandPalette.tsx`); ignored while typing and with a modifier held. Keys are the `shortcut` of each `NAV_SECTIONS` item, unique and required (`tests/go-shortcuts.test.cjs`); all 26 letters are used, so Cash forecast is `4`. The sidebar (desktop width) and the palette show them as "G H". Headless Edge never fetches a manifest, so check installability in an off-screen headed window (`Page.getInstallabilityErrors`). Test: `frontend/tests/desktop-app.test.cjs`.

## Today home (2026-11-11)

`/` renders Today directly (no redirect; same static page) and the mobile Today tab opens `/today`. This read-only landing page shows up to three open agent request links in inbox order (not an urgency ranking), recorded reading progress, and links into the Log screen ("Start your check-in" opens `/log`; the quick links Supplement / Habit / Weight and "Open check-in" open `/log#log-part-<section>`, see Log screen). Informational messages stay in the Agents inbox; Status stays available. Loading, failure, empty and unknown progress states remain distinct. Fetch on mount, manual refresh and return to the app; stale requests cannot replace newer results. Opening Today never sends a reply, marks anything read or logs a behavior. Reuse the existing authenticated inbox and reading endpoints, and keep personal record data out of review prompts and fixture tests. **Attention model (2026-11-16, `lib/today-attention.ts`, import-free):** every open item Today reads (requests, urgent email, experiment arm, soon-starting events, money, renewals, packages; reading as the one optional learning action) becomes an obligation keyed `<source>:<the source's own id>` (folded by that id only, never by wording: urgent email comes from `mergeUrgent(..., { bySearch: false })`, so a Gmail-search request is never folded into an email by matching words), ranked by urgency tier, due time, source, id with its reason, and every source carries a state so a checking, failed, stale or partial source is unknown, never a clear day; tests `frontend/tests/today-attention.test.cjs`. **Quiet view (2026-11-16, `lib/today-view.ts`, OFF by default):** the header's Quiet view toggle shows only the top N (1-5, default 3) obligations plus one optional reading action, urgent items past N with the reason, unconfirmed sources by name, and All open items one tap away; the full page stays in the DOM, hidden (shown again while personalizing); the setting is the UI preference `today.quiet` (written only on Riley's change, read at most every 15 min, applied on the next open; a device copy `onyx.today.quiet` paints it before React), and Snooze (to 06:00 next behavioral day) / Hide are device-only marks (`onyx.today.attention`, hashes) that close nothing and lapse when the item changes, and never outlive the item's own due time (`resurfaceAt`: the start of a date-only deadline's day, or 1 h before a request's expiry or an event's start; an item already due cannot be set aside; email, money and renewal dates are Eastern days, so their day boundaries and days left are counted in America/New_York, never in the device's zone); a money alert carries its own date (`MoneyAlert.due`; low cash: the first day the cautious path is below the line, `lowest` = the lowest day the title names, `likelyDue` = the first day the likely path is below it, which also makes it severity high) and is urgent when it is today or tomorrow (low cash: its likely day, or its own day when the likely path breaches); the renewals summary, like the money alerts, answers an older stored detection with `updating: true` + `asOf` and Today follows it up once with `?fresh=1`, never reading it as a confirmed "nothing renews"; an urgent email's deadline is its own day (`deadline.date`, Eastern, so a copy kept past midnight never moves it) and a request merged into it keeps its expiry (`TodayUrgentItem.cardExpires`), whichever comes first; today's experiment arm is pinned (always shown, never budgeted out, snoozed or hidden) and a sync that has not reported a run counts as unknown; tests `frontend/tests/today-view.test.cjs`, `tests/today-regression.cjs`.

**Overview: the whole app in one scroll (2026-11-14, `components/overview/`, `lib/overview-cards.ts`, `lib/overview-views.ts`).** Under Today's own panels (which stay first and unchanged) one card per area: recovery + tonight's HRV forecast, sleep, activity + steps, nutrition + caffeine, supplements, habits + reading, money (alerts + renewals, no amounts), orders, calendar + care, screen time, agents, NYC search, status, and "More pages" (every `lib/nav.ts` page no card covers, so a new page is listed the day it is added; a card of its own = an entry in `OVERVIEW_CARDS` + its sources in `components/overview/sources.ts` + its view in `lib/overview-views.ts`). Each card: up to 3 numbers, one small hand-drawn SVG chart or 2 lines, an Open link; one fixed height (272 px) shared with its skeleton and error state, so nothing moves. Speed: the overview is its own chunk (`TodayOverview.tsx`, `ssr:false`), a card reads only within 600 px of the screen (one IntersectionObserver) and mounts its body through `DeferredMount` (new `placeholder` prop); reads are existing GET endpoints and small `lib/queries` reads (7-9 days) through the client cache, URL keys shared with Today and the pages (a card waits up to 2.5 s for Today's own fetch of a shared key instead of asking twice), and the cards further down are read one per idle slot (10-minute freshness; also added to the warm-up's `/today` reads, memory-only and Today's own keys excluded). Privacy: finance and procurement come only from `/api/finance/alerts` and `/api/procurement/renewals/summary`, screen time only from the new `GET /api/screen-time/summary` (numbers only, service role via `lib/screen-time-server.ts`, owner session, not in `isCiRoute()`), all memory-only in the cache; care items memory-only too; medical records and the experiment arm are never read (link only). **Edit** (one self-contained component, `OverviewEditor.tsx`, ready to move onto a shared Personalize framework): a draft of the order with show / hide, drag by the grip on a mouse (accent insertion marker where the card will land), up / down buttons on the phone and for the keyboard (44 px), Reset to default, Cancel, Done. Only Done writes: the arrangement `{v: 1, order, hidden}` goes to `PUT /api/ui/preferences` under `overview.cards`, so phone and desktop match (re-read when the app returns after a minute); `mergeArrangement` keeps a saved order valid as cards are added or removed. Opening Today still writes nothing. Tests: `frontend/tests/overview-cards.test.cjs`; `tests/today-regression.cjs` renders the overview as nothing.

**Urgent email (2026-11-11).** Email only; text messages stay disabled. inbox-triage (personal-agents) decides what is urgent; Today only shows it. Two sources, merged into one list by `mergeUrgent` in `lib/urgent-email.ts`: (1) threads it labelled `Agent/Action` or `Agent/Deadline`, copied hourly by `urgent_email_etl.py` into `pds.inbox_emails` (service-role only; subject, sender, times, labels, flags and a ≤240-char snippet that never leaves the server) and served by `GET /api/urgent-email` (`lib/urgent-email-query.ts`: one item per thread; archived threads, Action older than 14 days, deadlines already passed or more than 7 days out, and undated Deadline mail older than 3 days are left out); (2) its `Urgent: …` Agent Inbox questions from `/onyx/inbox` (any card whose link is a Gmail URL counts too). A card whose link names a labelled thread (`#all/thread-f:<X-GM-THRID>`, same account) merges into that item, and the same Message-ID in both mailboxes shows once; email cards are listed under Urgent email, not under Needs your input. A card whose link is only a Gmail `#search/…` merges into the ONE labelled email of the same account, received in the 7 days before the card, that the search would find judged on subject and sender (`gmailSearchTerms` + `searchMatchesEmail`: each plain word in the subject or the sender's display name, or the domain labels when there is no name, never the address local part; `subject:` / `from:` / quoted phrases / `-` exclusions honoured, other operators ignored); two candidates = no merge. Each item shows its source, age and the label sync's age, why it is shown, and the next step, with an `Open in Gmail` link (mail.google.com only) and/or `Open request`. Until `sql/inbox_emails_schema.sql` is applied the API answers `not_connected` and Today says the label sync is not connected. `/status` card **Urgent Email** (badge GMAIL) = `urgent_email|labels` heartbeat age: >3 h degraded, >8 h or a failed run red. Tests: `frontend/scripts/test-urgent-email.mjs`, `tests/test_urgent_email_etl.py`, `tests/today-regression.cjs` (synthetic fixtures only).

## Procurement (2026-11-13)

`/procurement` gives **read-only advice, never buys**: no route, function or button places an order, opens a cart, checks out, pays or cancels (ordering / PunchOut was cancelled 2026-11-11; `executionReadiness()` always fails closed; do not revive `feat/procurement-punchout`). Everything is rule-based code: **no model or chat tool reads finance data**. Details: `docs/procurement-offline.md`.

- **Detected subscriptions + renewal alerts.** `lib/procurement-recurring-server.ts` (`server-only`, service role, explicit columns) reads 400 days of `pds.finance_transactions` per request; `lib/procurement-recurring.ts` (pure) groups counted USD purchases by merchant and accepts a cadence when the median gap fits a band (weekly 6-8 d, monthly 26-35, quarterly 84-98, annual 350-380), 70% of gaps are in it and the last three amounts agree within 25%. Each subscription gets an opaque `id` (hash of the normalized merchant), an estimated next charge date, the **method** (calendar = same day of the month, or a fixed interval, whichever fits the last four gaps better; median of the last three charges carried forward) and a **confidence** (high = 3+ gaps, none off by more than 2 days; medium = none off by more than 4; else low). Same-amount charges on no schedule are listed as irregular with **no date**. `lib/procurement-renewals.ts` decides what alerts: active subscriptions with high / medium confidence only. The page (`app/procurement/DetectedSubscriptions.tsx`) shows "Renewing soon" (14 days) and the method + confidence per row; the page stays `force-dynamic` + `noindex`, never ISR.
- **Today "Renewals" panel** renders only when a renewal is due within 3 days: `GET /api/procurement/renewals/summary` (owner session, `no-store`, GET only, nothing written; merchant + "renews tomorrow", **no amounts**; 500 on a failed ledger read, `{status:"empty"}` for an empty ledger). A failed check keeps the last answer and never shows an all-clear.
- **Waste flags** (`lib/procurement-flags.ts` pure + `lib/procurement-flags-server.ts` `server-only`), on ACTIVE subscriptions, each with its reason: **price went up** (last charge at least 5% and $0.50 above the usual amount of the up-to-three charges before it; one step up of at most double keeps the subscription detected), **possible duplicate** (two active subscriptions in one category of `SERVICE_CATEGORIES`, a short hand-edited list: add a service by adding a line), **no recent use on your iPhone** (only when the subscription is matched to an app / site in `pds.phone_usage_items` and the last 30 days total under 10 minutes with at least 20 days of Screen Time data; otherwise the row says "iPhone use: unknown (why)" and nothing is flagged; a failed or cut-off Screen Time read is unknown, never zero). Dismiss / Restore goes through `PATCH /api/procurement/flags` (`{sub, flag, dismissed}`; owner session, same-origin JSON, 256 bytes) into `pds.procurement_flag_dismissals` (`sql/procurement_flag_dismissals.sql`; service-role only; two hashes and a boolean). Flag keys carry the new amount / the other subscriptions, so a later increase or a new duplicate comes back.
- **Purchase suggestions** (`lib/procurement-reorder.ts` pure + `lib/procurement-reorder-server.ts` `server-only`; card `app/procurement/ReorderSuggestions.tsx`): from `pds.online_orders` item names (shipping emails + the Amazon order list, last 540 days; restaurant delivery is NOT read), an item ordered on 3+ separate occasions (orders within 3 days = one occasion) gets its median reorder interval and "likely due" (90% of the interval since the last order) / "due soon" (70%) / "not yet", with the reason; canceled orders never count, items ordered twice or not for 3+ intervals are only counted. The card has **no link, button or form** (a test fails if one is added): never add a cart or store link.
- **Renewal card in `/agents` (Riley's decision 2026-11-13: merchant name yes, amount never).** `POST /api/procurement/renewals/claim` is the **only** procurement path in `isCiRoute()`, exact match; the sms-reminders Worker polls it hourly (quiet 23:30-07:00) and posts "<merchant> renews in N days" (thread `renewals`, card type `renewal`, one "Open procurement" link, no buttons). `lib/procurement-renewal-claim.ts` claims active subscriptions renewing within 3 days (high / medium confidence, same rule as Today) with an atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING` into `pds.procurement_renewal_prompt` (`sql/procurement_renewal_prompts.sql`; service-role only; key = subscription hash + date of last charge = one billing cycle), after everything that can fail. **The response carries only `id`, `merchant`, `renewal_date`, `days`** (`CLAIM_FIELDS`); never add an amount, account, category or transaction id: `tests/procurement-renewal-claim.test.cjs` and `scripts/test-procurement-store.mjs` fail if a field or a second CI path is added.
