Heartbeat `procurement_renewals|prompt` (code-only error text, never a merchant) and `/status` card **Renewal Reminders** (>3 h degraded, >9 h or failed red). Every other procurement route and every finance route stays session-only.
- Hand-typed tracker (`pds.procurement_subscriptions`, service-role only, `/api/procurement/subscriptions`), `.ics` reminder file and in-memory order planner are unchanged.
- Tests (synthetic data only): `frontend/tests/procurement-recurring.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/procurement-renewals.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/procurement-flags.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/procurement-reorder.test.cjs`, `frontend/tests/procurement-renewal-claim.test.cjs`, `frontend/scripts/test-procurement*.mjs`, `tests/today-regression.cjs`. Keep real merchants, amounts and item names out of tests, logs, PR text and commit messages (public repo).

**Clearing (2026-11-14, `lib/today-clear.ts`).** "Needs your input" and "Urgent email" each have an × per row and a **Clear all** (every item the panel knows about, not only the rows on screen), then an 8-second **Undo** bar. Opening Today still clears nothing; only a tap does. Agent Inbox cards (requests, and inbox-triage questions merged into an urgent item: `TodayUrgentItem.clear.cards`) go through the Worker's `POST /onyx/dismiss`, the same call as the Agents inbox's Clear (the card leaves Unread and the badge; the agent still waits for an answer; Today also closes the phone's notifications). Labelled threads (`clear.emails`, including the same message's copy in the other mailbox) go to **`POST /api/urgent-email/clear`** (`{items: [{key, receivedAt}]}`, `undo: true` to bring them back; owner session only, never add it to `isCiRoute()`; same-origin JSON, 16 KB cap; logs a database code only) → **`pds.today_cleared_emails`** (`sql/today_cleared_emails.sql`, applied; service-role only; SHA-256 of the thread key + the newest message time cleared). `getUrgentEmail` leaves a cleared thread out (counted as `cleared`) until a NEWER message arrives in it, and shows everything if that read fails. Nothing is sent to Gmail: no label, read flag or archive change. Cleared rows stay hidden on screen while the servers catch up; a failed clear puts the row back with a message. Tests: `frontend/tests/today-clear.test.cjs`, `frontend/scripts/test-urgent-email.mjs`, `tests/today-regression.cjs` (`lib/today-clear.ts` is import-free and bundled by name there; the regression transpiles Today to ES5, so use `Array.from`, not spread, on a Set or Map).

**Calendar (2026-11-13).** A panel after Urgent email lists today's and tomorrow's Google Calendar events (read-only copies from `pds.calendar_events`, see the Google Calendar bullet under Database): all-day first, then by start time, times in the browser's zone, with the calendar label, "Tentative" where the calendar says so and the location; at most six per day. One `GET /api/calendar/events?from=<local today>&to=<local tomorrow>` per load, inside the same sequence guard as the other panels. Not connected (table missing, or no sync run and no events), an empty day, a failed check and a failed refresh (keeps the last list) are four different states; a sync older than 3 h or not `success` is flagged. The only link goes to `/health`. Helpers are value-imported from the import-free `lib/calendar-events.ts`, which `tests/today-regression.cjs` bundles by name (register any new value import there); the test's fetch mock answers the calendar URL at once and keeps it out of `window.calls` / `window.pending`.

## PC activity logger (2026-11-14)

`tools/pc_activity_logger/activity_logger.py` (README there) runs on Riley's PC as one hidden process. Every 30 s it keeps two things: seconds since the last input (`GetLastInputInfo`, a timestamp only) and the foreground program's executable name. Never window titles, URLs, document names, keystrokes, the clipboard or the screen; `tests/test_pc_activity_aggregation.py` fails if the source gains one of those APIs. Active = input within 3 min and the screen not locked. Samples stay in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Onyx\pc-activity` (removed 30 days after upload). Every hour each pending day is upserted to `pds.pc_activity_hours` / `pds.pc_activity_apps` (`sql/pc_activity_schema.sql`, service-role only; view `pds.pc_activity_daily` per behavioral day with late 21:00+ and per-program minutes) over PostgREST with `~/.onyx_supabase_secret`, plus a `pc_activity|usage` heartbeat through `sync_log_helper.log_sync` on every upload and on a crash. `/status` card "PC Activity" (>26 h degraded, >72 h or a failed row red). Standard library only: no pip install on the PC. **Not built yet:** check-in pre-fills from it ("Worked late?", "BI/Python/AL/Excel"), because the program-to-habit mapping and the late threshold need Riley's decision and some real data. The iPhone place-visits half of the same roadmap row needs iOS app work and a device test.

## AI tool usage harvester (2026-11-14)

`tools/ai_usage_harvest/ai_usage_harvest.py` (README there) runs on Riley's PC every 3 h as a hidden scheduled task ("Onyx AI Usage Harvest", `pythonw.exe`). It reads the session logs Claude Code (`~/.claude/projects/<project>/<session>.jsonl`, subagent transcripts nested under `<session>/`) and Codex (`~/.codex/sessions/**/*.jsonl[.zst]`) keep on the PC. **Privacy is the main requirement:** those logs hold whole conversations and possibly secrets, so each line is parsed in memory and only its time, record / block types, model name, token counts and (once per session) the working folder's NAME are kept. Never prompt or reply text, tool inputs or outputs, paths, branches, titles or ids. Rows are built from an allow-list and checked by `check_row` before upload; a failing row stops the upload with a `GUARD privacy:` failed heartbeat; errors are logged as their type name only. `tests/test_ai_usage_harvest.py` feeds synthetic logs whose every content field carries a marker and fails if it reaches the rows, the dry-run output, the upload, the heartbeat or the log. Never put real log lines in a test or a fixture.

One row per (device, behavioral day, tool, project) in `pds.ai_tool_usage_daily` (`sql/ai_tool_usage_schema.sql`, owner read + service role; **not applied yet**), upserted over PostgREST with `~/.onyx_supabase_secret` (it imports the PC activity logger's `Rest` client), plus an `ai_usage|sessions` heartbeat through `sync_log_helper.log_sync` on every run (`partial` = some files unreadable). `/status` card "AI Tool Usage" (heartbeat only; >26 h degraded, >72 h or failed red). Rules: top-level sessions only count as sessions / prompts / active minutes (subagents add tokens, tool calls and a subagent count); SDK (`sdk-*`) and Codex `exec` runs are left out; records repeated by resumed or forked sessions count once; behavioral day in the PC's zone; Codex times come from its turn / item events because the desktop app sometimes writes a turn's lines later in one batch. A day is re-sent until it has ended + 1 h, then final (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Onyx\ai-usage\state.json`), so Claude Code deleting logs after ~30 days never shrinks a stored day; Claude Code days older than 25 days are not sent. Riley's steps: apply the SQL, then `pwsh -File tools\ai_usage_harvest\register-task.ps1`. **Not built yet:** any page or check-in use of the data. Tests: `tests/test_ai_usage_harvest.py`, `frontend/tests/ai-usage-status.test.cjs`.

## Screen Time page (2026-11-13)

`/screen-time` shows the Screen Time app's data (`pds.phone_usage_daily`, see "Phone screen time v2"): daily total, late-night minutes split at midnight, pickups + notifications, estimated minutes in the hour before WHOOP sleep onset and after it, top apps and top categories, for the last 14 / 30 / 90 days (`?days=`, 7 to 180, default 30). The view is service-role only, so the page follows the `/financials` pattern, NOT the ISR pattern: `force-dynamic`, `noindex`, read only by `lib/screen-time-server.ts` (`server-only`, explicit column list, logs an error code only) behind the owner-only middleware; the chart component gets its rows as props and the browser never queries the view. Failed query, empty window and data are three different screens. Pure logic in `lib/screen-time.ts`, mirroring the pipeline's readiness rules: a day is **complete** once a sync happened after it ended (`synced_at` >= day + 1 at 06:00 ET; the newest day is usually partial, drawn dimmed and left out of every average and top list), and the two sleep-anchored estimates are shown only when the day is complete, the WHOOP sleep is scored and the sync is at least an hour past sleep onset. Days the app never reported are gaps, never zeros. The export carries bundle ids, not app names (`phone_usage_items.name` is NULL), so top apps show a readable name derived from the id (`appLabel`) with the id beside it. Smoke: in `PAGES` with `legitimateEmpty` (a month without a sync skips; the failed-query text is a hard fail). Tests: `frontend/tests/screen-time.test.cjs` (synthetic rows; also renders the three states offline).

## Experiments: N-of-1 trials (2026-11-13)

Design, Riley's decisions and the build status are in `docs/experiment-framework.md` (Sections 13 and 12.1). **No trial is registered; starting one is Riley's action, never an agent's.** What exists:

- **Tables** (`sql/experiment_framework_schema.sql`, applied): `pds.experiments` (one pre-registered protocol per row), `pds.experiment_secrets` (the 32-byte seed), `pds.experiment_schedule` (one arm per behavioral day), `pds.experiment_days` (self-reported adherence and declared exclusions), view `pds.experiment_day_status` (past days only, with the objective Screen Time check). **Service role only** (RLS on, no policies, no grants to anon / authenticated), and the service role cannot remove rows. Trial adherence never goes in `pds.habit_journal` (a habit row would become a treatment in `causal_inference.py`), and the arm never enters the behavioral matrix.
- **Concealment is the point of the access model.** The schedule for the whole trial exists from registration, but the app can learn only today's arm: the single reveal is `pds.experiment_today()` (called by `lib/experiments-server.ts`, `server-only`), which returns the arm of `pds.behavioral_today_now()` and stamps `first_revealed_at` once. The check-in question's `pds.experiment_adherence_day()` repeats an arm only for today or yesterday and only once that stamp exists. The frontend calls these functions only, never a table. Never add a query on `experiment_schedule` or `experiment_secrets` to the frontend, never add `/api/experiments/*` to `isCiRoute()`, and never print a seed or an arm in a script (Actions logs are public). `frontend/tests/experiments.test.cjs` fails on each of these.
- **The protocol is locked at registration** (trigger `experiments_guard`): afterwards only the status (forward only), the stop fields and the Notion page id can change; seed and schedule never. One active trial at a time. A wrong registration is stopped and redone under a new id, not edited.
- **Libraries (pure, no I/O):** `experiment_design.py` (permuted blocks from PCG64's raw stream, seed commitment, protocol validation: MID of 8 ms or more, first day in the future, alignment version must equal `hrv_alignment.ALIGNMENT_VERSION`) and `experiment_analysis.py` (model with the carryover term, randomization test, interval by inversion, decision rule). `analyze_trial` raises `AnalysisNotAllowed` before the pre-registered end: do not add a way around it, and do not compute an effect on a running trial by any other route (Riley's decision 1). The same-night and carryover p-values / intervals allow for one-night carryover (joint sharp null + Berger-Boos; the intervals are certified enclosures of every accepted value; `p_value_no_effect` = Fisher's no-effect test, which still needs the schedule, missing nights not caused by the arm and an end fixed in advance and is what the A/A check reads; nulls and assumptions per output in doc 8.1, mirrored in the report's `inference` block with `supported` per output), and `decision` stays `inconclusive` (verdict in `decision_if_supported`) while arm-dependent missing nights, a stop before the planned end (whatever its reason: no registered stopping rule is calibrated), a protocol without `analysis.carryover_nights: 1` + `model_justification` or with another `inference_method`, or a `METHOD_REVIEW` (the seeded report of `experiment_calibration.py`, doc 8.2, plus an independent review) that does not cover the trial's alpha, re-draw count and block design withhold it; the register step refuses a treatment trial until that review exists. The independent review is recorded (doc 8.3, 2026-11-16: two non-Anthropic models, final verdicts sound; it reviews the method, never a trial's outcome); the calibration report is not, so every final report still withholds its decision. Both halves of `METHOD_REVIEW` are structured records bound to the code by `experiment_fingerprint.py` (a syntax-tree hash of the analysis and the allocation / re-draw code, docstrings and the output explanations left out; `python experiment_fingerprint.py` prints it): any change to that code withholds every decision until both are recorded again; tests `tests/test_experiment_calibration.py`.
- **Admin command:** `experiment_admin.py` (`seed`, `register`, `verify`, `stop`, `status`, `power`), run only through the manual workflow `experiment-admin.yml` on `master` (`gh workflow run experiment-admin.yml -f action=...`; the four steps to start a trial are in the doc, Section 12.1). Protocol files live in `docs/experiments/` (`TEMPLATE.md`; `EXP-000` can never be registered). Heartbeat `experiments|admin` on every write; a schedule that no longer matches its seed is a `GUARD experiment_schedule:` failure.
- **Today card.** When an N-of-1 trial is running, Today shows that behavioral day's arm and its instruction at the top (`GET /api/experiments/today`). With no trial registered, which is the usual case, nothing is rendered. The card has no buttons; opening Today records nothing about what Riley did.
- **Check-in question (2026-11-14).** While a trial runs, `/check-in` asks "Followed today's arm?" (Yes / Partly / No + an optional note) on the Today tab, and on the Yesterday tab for an answer given the next morning (`app/check-in/ExperimentAdherence.tsx`, `GET|POST /api/experiments/adherence`). Each tap saves at once (on its own, not through the check-in's answers) into `pds.experiment_days`, only through the database functions `pds.experiment_adherence_day()` / `pds.experiment_record_adherence()` (`sql/experiment_adherence_reports_schema.sql`). They answer only for a running trial, only for today or yesterday, and only for a day whose arm Today has already shown (`first_revealed_at`); otherwise "open Today to see today's arm" or nothing. The check-in never calls `/api/experiments/today` (only Today reveals). With no trial it renders nothing.
- **Report job (2026-11-14):** `experiment_report.py` (`--dry-run` is its only option; there is deliberately no `--as-of` / `--force`). With no trial it is a no-op with a success heartbeat. While a trial runs it writes PROGRESS counts only to `pds.experiment_reports` (days, nights with an outcome, missing share, adherence from `experiment_adherence.py`: the Screen Time check decides once settled, the check-in answer is the fallback) and never loads an HRV value; from the planned end + 2 days it writes the FINAL report once (the registered analysis plus covariate, Eight Sleep and resting-HR fits, flight / transition / declared-pause block exclusions, the seed reveal) and marks the trial complete. The table's guard refuses an effect in a progress row and a final row before the end. Heartbeat `experiments|report` on every run (`partial` = 3+ nights in a row without HRV or adherence under the protocol's `adherence.min_pct`); `GUARD experiment_alignment:` / `GUARD experiment_schedule:` are `failed`. `/status` has an **Experiments** card on that heartbeat (Unknown until the first run). The scheduled workflow (`.github/workflows/experiment-report.yml`, daily 15:00 UTC, env `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `NOTION_API_KEY`) and its `tools/watchdog_cloud.py` `SCHEDULED` line are in the held PR "[Riley merge] Experiment report schedule", merged by Riley after the SQL file is applied.
- **Notion mirror (2026-11-14):** `experiment_notion.py`, called by the report job through `experiment_report.Mirror`. One page per experiment in **"Onyx Experiments"** (data source `d99f3df8-6759-4de0-b9be-3761759ecd26`, under Project Onyx; doc 10.1): protocol fields + Status from registration on, progress counts while running (the progress writer cannot send an effect field), the effect fields and the "Report (auto)" toggle section only from the final report (replaced, never appended; Riley's own blocks stay). Found by `pds.experiments.notion_page_id`, else by title; two pages for one id are refused. Same secret and client conventions as the other Notion jobs (`NOTION_API_KEY`, httpx + `retry_helper.retry_http`, Notion-Version `2025-09-03` as in `tools/notion_tasks.py`). **Best-effort:** the Supabase row is written first; a Notion failure or a missing key turns the heartbeat `partial` (status + Notion error code only), and a final report that missed Notion is retried from the stored payload (`notion_synced_at` empty), never re-analysed. Not called with no trial registered or in `--dry-run`.
- **A/A check (2026-11-14):** protocol field `kind: aa` (default `treatment`). `validate_protocol` requires identical arms and no `adherence.objective` for `aa`, and two different arms for a treatment trial. `docs/experiments/EXP-900.md` is the 4-week check (7 blocks; **defined, not seeded or registered**; real trials count up from EXP-001, A/A checks from EXP-900). Its final report gets `aa_check` (no false effect at alpha, every day revealed, check-in answers on 90%+ of days); a check that does not pass turns that run's heartbeat `partial`. `aa_check` is one of the effect keys the report table's guard refuses in a progress row. Riley's steps: doc 12.2.
- **Not built yet:** declaring a pause from the app, interim looks, the period design. `sql/experiment_adherence_reports_schema.sql` is **not applied yet** (Riley); `experiment_admin.py register` refuses until it is.
- Tests: `python tests/test_experiment_framework.py`, `python tests/test_experiment_report.py`, `python tests/test_experiment_notion.py`, `cd frontend && node --test tests/experiments.test.cjs tests/checkin-client.test.cjs`, `node tests/today-regression.cjs` (synthetic only).

## Health & appointments (2026-11-13)

`/health` is a care planner (due-soon list, month / agenda calendar with projected repeats), not a medical record. Items are stored in **`pds.care_items`** (`sql/care_items_schema.sql`): label, kind, calendar date + optional wall-clock time + the IANA zone that time is in (stored as written, never converted, so 9:00 stays 9:00 across a DST change), "repeat N months after completion", note, booked / archived / last-completed, `removed_at` (soft removal; the app never physically removes a row) and `import_key`. **Service-role only** (RLS on, no policies, nothing granted to anon / authenticated): the page is `force-dynamic` (no ISR, `noindex`) and reads through `lib/care-items-server.ts` (`server-only`); the browser writes through **`/api/care/items`** (GET, POST), **`/api/care/items/[id]`** (PATCH, DELETE = soft removal, PATCH `{removed:false}` = Undo) and **`/api/care/items/import`** (the one-time, idempotent copy of the old browser-only planner, keyed on `import_key`). The continuous scroll's `/health` section (`app/health/FeedSection.tsx`) renders the same `HealthClient` from **`GET /api/care/page-data`** (exactly what `page.tsx` passes: `{initial, calendar}`; same-origin, no-store, kept in memory only by the client cache). Those routes are owner-session only (never add `/api/care` to `isCiRoute()`), same-origin, JSON, size-capped, strict about unknown fields; errors and logs carry a database code only, never item text, and nothing is written to `sync_log`. The add / edit form autosaves (`lib/autosave.ts`, 2026-11-15): a new item is created once it has a label and a valid date and Riley pauses, later edits update it, Done starts a new item, Undo takes the whole edit back. A create retried after a lost answer gets the stored row and sends newer edits as an update. Switching to another item never drops an unsaved or incomplete entry (the form says so); Clear empties a new entry. Writes are optimistic per item and revert only that item on failure (`applyOp` / `revertOp` / `inverseOp` in `lib/care-items.ts`); load failure, empty list and data are three different states. Date, repeat and time-zone logic is pure in `lib/care-planner.ts`. Sample data is display-only and is refused by the API. Not an ingestion source, so no `/status` card; Today is not wired. Docs: `docs/health-appointments.md`. Tests (synthetic only): `cd frontend && node scripts/test-care-planner.mjs && node --test tests/care-items.test.cjs`.

**Google Calendar sync (built 2026-11-13, NOT connected until Riley sets it up; steps in `docs/health-google-calendar.md`).** One way, Onyx to Google, with exactly ONE scope, `calendar.app.created` (Onyx can only touch calendars it created: never Riley's own calendars, never Gmail; do not add a scope). Onyx creates one calendar ("Onyx care") and keeps one event per active care item (label, date / time / zone, booked state; notes are never sent; no recurring events). Pure logic in `lib/care-google.ts` (`eventForItem`, `runSync` against a `CalendarApi` interface); server part in `lib/care-google-server.ts`; routes `/api/care/google/{status,start,callback,sync,disconnect}` are owner-session only. **Nothing calls Google unless `GOOGLE_CALENDAR_CLIENT_ID` + `GOOGLE_CALENDAR_CLIENT_SECRET` exist in Vercel AND a refresh token is stored** (`pds.ci_tokens`, `service='google_calendar'`); without the env vars there is no database call either. Sign-in is authorization code + PKCE with a `state` bound to a 10-minute httpOnly cookie scoped to the callback path; a grant with any other scope is revoked and not stored. Every event carries the private extended property `onyxCareItem=<item id>`, and an event is read and that tag checked before any rewrite or removal, so Onyx never edits an event it did not create. State lives in `pds.care_calendar_sync` (one row: calendar id, connected-at, last result as counts + a short code) and `pds.care_calendar_links` (item to event id, generation, digest; rows are kept and marked `gone`), both service-role only (`sql/care_calendar_sync_schema.sql`). A sync runs via `after()` on every care-item write, from Sync now, and from the **scheduled catch-up** `POST /api/jobs/care-calendar-catch-up` (2026-11-14: every 6 h; the route checks `x-onyx-ci-token` itself, so a browser session alone cannot run it, then calls the same `syncCareCalendar()`; it answers counts + a short code, 200 `{ran:false}` while not set up / not connected, 502 on a failed run). The schedule reaches it only once Riley adds its ONE exact path to middleware's `isCiRoute()` and the workflow `care-calendar-catch-up.yml` (both protected paths; exact text on the roadmap row); until then nothing calls it. Results are not written to `sync_log`: the `/status` card **Care Calendar** (`care_calendar`; logic in `lib/care-calendar-status.ts`, pure) reads only the `pds.care_calendar_sync` row (never `ci_tokens`, never an item, and nothing at all until the Google variables exist): unknown while not set up or not connected, red on a failed sync (with what to do), degraded after 14 h and red after 30 h without any sync. Tests: `cd frontend && node --test tests/care-google.test.cjs tests/status-heartbeats.test.cjs` (fake Google + in-memory database).

## Medical records (2026-11-14)

`/health/records` (nav: Medical records) stores **lab results** (test, number or text value, unit, reference range, collection date, lab, optional source document; grouped by test + unit with the latest value, High / Low / In range against the typed range only (`<` / `>` exclude the bound, `<=` `≤` `>=` `≥` and a span include it; stored as `ref_low_strict` / `ref_high_strict`), change since last, and a trend chart from two numeric results) and **documents** (PDF / JPEG / PNG / WebP / HEIC up to 25 MB with title, kind, date of service, provider, note). Tables `pds.medical_documents` + `pds.lab_results` and the storage bucket `medical-records` (`sql/medical_records_schema.sql`, applied) are **service-role only**: RLS on, no policies, no anon / authenticated grants, no `storage.objects` policy, private bucket with its own size and type limits. The page is `force-dynamic` + `noindex` and reads through `lib/medical-records-server.ts` (`server-only`); the browser uses `/api/medical/documents` (GET, POST = details + one-time signed upload token), `/api/medical/documents/[id]` (PATCH, DELETE = soft), `/api/medical/documents/[id]/complete` (server checks the file's size and type, then marks it `ready`; only ready documents are listed or served), `/api/medical/documents/[id]/file` (303 to a 60-second signed link, `?download=1` to save), `/api/medical/labs` (GET, POST a batch of up to 60, idempotent on browser-picked ids) and `/api/medical/labs/[id]` (PATCH, DELETE = soft). The continuous scroll's `/health/records` section (`app/health/records/FeedSection.tsx`) renders the same `RecordsClient` from **`GET /api/medical/page-data`** (`{documents, labs}` exactly as `page.tsx` passes them; same-origin, no-store, a memory-only key in the client cache). Files go straight from the browser to the bucket because Vercel caps request bodies at ~4.5 MB. **Never add `/api/medical` to `isCiRoute()`, never let a chat tool or model read these tables or files, never log record text** (errors carry a database code only; nothing goes to `sync_log`). Entry is by hand: reading a document with Claude would send it to Anthropic and needs Riley's decision first. Removal is soft everywhere and the file stays for Undo. Editing a lab result or a document's details autosaves (PATCH after a pause or on blur; Done closes). Adding lab results stays a batch with Save results. Not an ingestion source: no `/status` card. Smoke: in `PAGES` with `legitimateEmpty`. Docs: `docs/medical-records.md`. **Backups: Supabase's database backups do not hold the bucket's files**; RPO / RTO, the encrypted off-site plan (option A chosen: a PC job to OneDrive, not built yet) and the synthetic-file drill are in `docs/medical-records-recovery.md`, and `tools/medical_recovery/recovery_check.py` is the read-only, counts-only check (it never downloads a file from production). Tests (synthetic only): `cd frontend && node --test tests/medical-records.test.cjs`, `python tests/test_medical_recovery_check.py`.

## Financials (2026-11-12; SimpleFIN feed since 2026-11-14)

`/financials` reads a persisted finance ledger: `pds.finance_accounts`, `pds.finance_transactions` (`txn_key`; **`amount_cents` is expense-positive**, purchases +, income and refunds -; a non-empty `ignored_from` excludes the row) and `pds.finance_balance_snapshots` (per account, `captured_at`, `balance_cents`, `kind` asset|liability|null). **Since 2026-11-14 the feed is SimpleFIN Bridge** (`simplefin_etl.py`, next paragraph); everything before the cutover came from Rocket Money (CSV export + a browser harvester, now retired) and stays as it is (`finance_transactions.source` = `rocketmoney` | `simplefin`). **Rocket Money rows are keyed on Rocket Money's own transaction id (`source_txn_id`, `sql/finance_source_txn_id.sql`, partial unique index), not on the name or the amount**: the daily harvester reads the Transactions list from inside the signed-in app (no email, no export button; pending rows skipped) and upserts by that id, attaching the id to an export-origin row with the same (account, date, amount) instead of inserting a twin, or inserting with `txn_key` = sha256(`rmid|`+id) first 32 hex; the 7,009 export rows were tied to their ids once by `tools/rocketmoney_harvest/reconcile-ids.mjs` (6,997 matched; the n-th row of a same-day group in the file takes the n-th smallest id). `import-csv.mjs` stays for manual backfills and inserts only the surplus of CSV rows per (account, date, amount) group so a re-import cannot duplicate web rows. **The tables are SERVICE-ROLE ONLY** (RLS on, no policies, no grants to anon/authenticated, like `phone_usage_*`): the browser never queries them, the only reader is `lib/finance-ledger-server.ts` (`server-only`, service role, explicit column lists, 1000-row count-driven paging) called from the server page behind the owner-only middleware, no chat/LLM tool reads finance data, and nothing logs rows (errors log a code only). **The page is `force-dynamic`, deliberately NOT ISR** (private data: no CDN or shared cache, `noindex`); do not copy the ISR pattern here. Logic is pure and unit-tested in `lib/finance-ledger.ts`: money in/out exclude the one `NON_SPENDING_CATEGORIES` constant (Internal Transfers, Credit Card Payment, Savings Transfer) and ignored rows; refunds count as money in; net worth subtracts liabilities by absolute value; a balance with `kind` null is shown as unclassified and left out (never guessed from the account type); non-USD is never mixed into USD totals; balances older than 48 h get a stale badge. The page keeps empty ledger, failed query and data as three different states. `/status` card **SimpleFIN** (badge SIMPLEFIN, automated, `lib/finance-status.ts`, also the `/financials` freshness line): worst of the `simplefin|transactions` and `simplefin|balances` heartbeats, more than 14 h degraded, more than 30 h or a failed run red with the ETL's own text; heartbeats only, no amounts reach that cacheable endpoint. The Rocket Money card was removed with the harvester (its old `rocketmoney` heartbeats keep their history label). The browser-memory CSV workspace (PR #28) stays as a secondary "Preview a CSV" disclosure; it does not touch the ledger. Keep real finance data out of tests, screenshots, PR text and logs (the repo is public). Tests: `cd frontend && node --test tests/finance-ledger.test.cjs tests/finance-ledger-view.test.cjs tests/finance-ledger-server.test.cjs && node scripts/test-financials.mjs`.

**SimpleFIN Bridge feed (2026-11-14, `simplefin_etl.py`, `simplefin-etl.yml`, `sql/finance_simplefin.sql`, doc `docs/financials-simplefin.md`).** Replaced the Rocket Money harvester because its web sign-in expired within hours every day (no long-lived session, no refresh token on disk). SimpleFIN Bridge (beta-bridge.simplefin.org, $15/yr, Riley's account) aggregates the banks; Onyx holds only its read-only **Access URL** (credentials inside the URL) in the GitHub secret **`SIMPLEFIN_ACCESS_URL`** and `~/.simplefin_access_url` on the PC. One-time setup: Riley connects the banks on SimpleFIN's site, saves a setup token to `~/.simplefin_setup_token`, then `python simplefin_etl.py --claim --set-github-secret` (a setup token works once). Runs every 6 h at :30 (one `GET /accounts?version=2`, 45-day window (SimpleFIN warns above 45), max 90; SimpleFIN allows 24 requests a day and disables the Access URL beyond that, so do not raise the cadence). Coverage (2026-11-14): Amex, Wells Fargo, Citi, Discover, Fidelity daily; **Apple Card monthly** ("Updated Monthly"); **Apple Cash not offered** (Apple exposes it only on-device via FinanceKit). Rules (all pure, `tests/test_simplefin_etl.py`): pending rows are not requested; amounts flipped to expense-positive; dates = UTC date of `transacted_at`, else `posted`. **Account matching** (`finance_accounts.simplefin_id`): stored link, else institution + last four, else institution + name; an account that could be an existing one but is not unique is **skipped and reported** (heartbeat `partial`), never guessed; a new account gets its own row with a type inferred from its name, and an Investment or Unknown account is **balances-only** (trades and dividends are not spending). **Cutover** per account: SimpleFIN rows dated more than 3 days before the last Rocket Money row are dropped, rows near it are dropped only when an unclaimed Rocket Money row with the same amount sits within 3 days (Rocket Money never stored pending rows, so those are kept), rows already written by SimpleFIN are rewritten in place. **Categories** (SimpleFIN sends none; `/financials` needs them to keep payments and transfers out of spending): a same-amount, opposite-sign pair on two of Riley's accounts within 5 days with payment / transfer wording on one side -> Credit Card Payment (a card on either side) or Internal Transfers (Zelle / Venmo / Cash App / PayPal never pair); else the category Rocket Money gave the same description, merchant name or first two words in the last 3 years (80-85% agreement); else payment / transfer wording; else none. Balance snapshots: `captured_at` = SimpleFIN's balance-date, liabilities stored as a positive amount owed (like the Rocket Money history), `kind` from the account's Rocket Money snapshots, NULL for a new account. **Output is counts only** (public Actions logs): never names, descriptions, amounts, ids or the URL; `--dry-run` (local only) prints the per-account matching for checking. Heartbeats `simplefin|transactions` + `simplefin|balances` on every run: `failed` (exit 1) when SimpleFIN cannot be read (403 = Access URL revoked or disabled: new setup token + `--claim --set-github-secret`; 402 = subscription lapsed), `partial` with SimpleFIN's own connection message (e.g. a bank needs reconnecting on SimpleFIN's site) or a skipped account. Before the secret exists a scheduled run writes failed heartbeats and exits 0.

**Rocket Money harvester: RETIRED 2026-11-14.** `tools/rocketmoney_harvest/` and its Task Scheduler entry "Onyx Rocket Money Harvest" (disabled, not deleted) stay as the record of how the history was built and for a manual backfill (`import-csv.mjs`); the sign-in notes are in its README. Do not re-enable it as a feed: its sign-in lasted hours, not days.

## Cash forecast, budgets and taxes (2026-11-14)

Design: `docs/cash-flow-forecast.md` (backtest numbers in Section 9.1). Pages: `/financials/forecast`, `/financials/budgets` (both `force-dynamic` + `noindex`, server-rendered, never ISR) and a **Money** panel on Today (`GET /api/finance/alerts`). **Read-only: nothing moves money, pays or files anything.** All tables are service-role only (RLS on, no policies, no grants): `pds.finance_plan_items` (planned items, balance-positive cents, soft remove), `pds.finance_plan_drivers` (keys `settings`, `tax` = the tax profile, `card:<account>`, `growth:<account>` or `growth:virtual-<slug>` for a planned account such as the SEP IRA, `scenario:<slug>`), `pds.finance_category_rules` + `pds.finance_txn_categories` (category fixes, applied when the ledger is read by `loadCategoryFixes`; stored rows untouched), `pds.finance_budgets` (`__total__` = overall), `pds.finance_forecast_snapshots` (weekly). Routes `/api/finance/{forecast/*,budgets,categories,alerts}` are owner-session only: **never add them to `isCiRoute()`**.
- **Engine** `lib/cash-forecast.ts` (pure, seeded, no clock; config `lib/cash-forecast-config.ts`): cash pool = Cash accounts (checking + savings); streams by schedule with US bank-holiday rules; card payments = each card's MONTHLY TOTAL paid (mean of the last 3 months), also for payments that cannot be paired with the card's own rows (per payee); everyday baseline = mean weekly net flow of what is left (only unusually large money IN is left out; rows the budget ignores are kept); block-bootstrap low/high bands (backtest: ~80% coverage); planned items matched to actuals (late money in dropped, late money out kept); 0% promo end milestones (dates read from balance-transfer row text `EXPIRES MM/YYYY`, else the `card:` driver); net worth 24 months (cards with APR/promo, investments at an assumed 5% plus contributions). Backtest: `cash-forecast-backtest.yml` (manual, prints percentages only).
- **Everything editable on the page**: detected streams (paycheck, card payments, bills) via drivers `adjust:<streamId>` ({from, amountCents} or {from, stop}); forecast settings in the one `settings` driver (bufferCents, inflationPct, lookbackWeeks, cardMonths, lowPercentile; every save merges); planned items repeat monthly or yearly (stored as month x12); tax rules in the profile (bracketIndexPct for unpublished federal years, default 2.7%; gaRatePct / ncRatePct overrides; employerStateWithholding NC | none). The move, investment-account rows, card APR / promo, forecast settings and tax profile autosave after a pause or on blur, then the page re-reads (`lib/use-finance-autosave.ts`). Adding a planned item, a goal or a planned account, recording a tax payment, and the stream Set amount / Stop / Reset stay buttons.
- **Every year's bonus**: the tax profile's `annualBonus` (amount, profit share, growth %, first plan year) generates a bonus + profit share each January for 4 plan years (`allOneOffs`; an explicit January "bonus" one-off replaces that year's); `raisePct` grows salary each January (tax wages and the paycheck stream). The tax plan covers this year and the next two, so a January bonus received as a NY/NYC resident is taxed there.
- **Taxes** `lib/tax-plan.ts` + `lib/tax-tables.ts`: federal, Georgia resident period, NC nonresident share (credit in Georgia), NY part-year (income-percentage method) + NYC; employer withholds NC only; estimated payments, refunds and after-tax bonuses become forecast lines (`tax:*`, `pay:*`). **Tax years are picked by cash date, not calendar year** (`taxYearsFor`, 2026-11-14): every year with a line inside the two-year window, and LAST year stays in after New Year until its January estimate, April balance and refund are more than the late-match window (31 days) behind; a tax line whose date passed is kept for that window, so the engine matches it to the payment or refund, or keeps counting an unpaid balance as of tomorrow (recording it in `estimatesPaid` settles it). Never go back to "this year + N": a year's January and April lines vanished on Jan 1 (Codex #168). **Every table names its basis (2026-11-16, PIPE-10):** `published` (source URL, check date, effective years: federal 2026 + 2027, Georgia 2026 4.99% / $15,000, NC by S.L. 2026-41, NY 2026 and 2027-2032 with the 601(d-1) recapture), `assumption` (federal 2028+ indexed by `bracketIndexPct`, also when Riley entered the index; Georgia 2027+ until OPB's Dec 1 report is entered) or `override` (a Georgia / NC rate Riley entered); an assumption past its `staleAfter` date shows a visible line on that year, and tables unchecked for a year (`TABLES_CHECKED`) one line above the years. The yearly refresh is owned by the Onyx Roadmap row "[TAX-REFRESH] Refresh built-in tax tables" (due each December 15). Part-year: wages and other income split at the move date by receipt date; the rounded NYC months prorate only the NYC standard deduction. Due dates move like IRC 7503 (`taxDueDate`). Planning estimates, not filing.
- **What-if** `lib/cash-forecast-scenario.ts`: levers in the URL, recomputed on the server (the browser never receives transactions); saved scenarios = drivers `scenario:<slug>`. The other-income lever is a change against the tax profile's own figure from `otherIncomeStart` (default: next month), received as one twelfth at each month end (`wi:other:*` lines) and taxed in the year it is received (`TaxProfile.otherIncomeChange`, `otherIncomeFor`), so its cash and its tax agree; the profile's own other income is taken as already in the history and adds no receipts.
- **Budgets** `lib/finance-budgets.ts`: spending = counted rows after category fixes (card payments and transfers never count); pace, projected month end, suggested = median of 3 months; uncategorized merchants are filed as soon as a category is picked (merchant rule); budget amounts autosave (the overall field: empty changes nothing, 0 clears it). **Alerts** `lib/finance-alerts.ts`: low cash on the cautious path within 30 days, budget over / heading over, 0% promo within 60 days, tax payment within 14 days. `GET /api/finance/alerts` answers from the stored result (`pds.finance_result_cache`) with `asOf` (when computed), `updating: true` when it is an older stored answer being recomputed after the response (inputs changed, a new ET day, a new build), and `partial` (`forecast` | `budgets`) when one read failed (never stored). Today says so under the alerts and asks once more after 4 s with `?fresh=1` (computes before answering unless the stored answer is current), so an older answer is never shown as current and a new alert appears within seconds; an older empty answer that could not be updated, or a partial one, shows the panel with that note instead of looking like an all-clear (`lib/money-alerts-view.ts`, import-free, bundled by name in `tests/today-regression.cjs`; Codex #191 D5).
- **Snapshot** `cash-forecast-snapshot.yml` (Mondays 12:30 UTC) → `frontend/scripts/cash-forecast-snapshot.cjs` → heartbeat `finance_forecast|snapshot`, `/status` card **Cash Forecast** (>8 days degraded, >15 days or failed red); the page shows forecast vs actual from the snapshots.
- **Plan & assumptions** on `/financials/forecast` (`PlanConfig.tsx`, `lib/finance-plan.ts`): the NYC move as driver `move` (date, rent now, NYC rent, moving costs, deposit, deposit refund, living-cost change; it is the single source of the move date and overrides the tax profile's), each investment account's return / monthly contribution / start (drivers `growth:<account>` and planned accounts `growth:virtual-<slug>` such as the SEP IRA or an HSA), and inflation (`settings.inflationPct`). Seeded values are marked as estimates.
- **Subscriptions + cancel tracker** on `/financials/budgets` (`lib/finance-subscriptions.ts`, `pds.finance_cancel_marks`, `PUT /api/finance/subscriptions`): Riley cancels on the merchant's site and marks it; the page reports waiting / stopped / still charging from the ledger. Onyx never cancels. Feature comparison: `docs/finance-vs-rocket-money.md`.
- **Recategorize** any transaction on `/financials` ("This one" = `finance_txn_categories`, "All from this merchant" = `finance_category_rules`). **Savings goals** on `/financials/forecast` (`pds.finance_goals`, `lib/finance-goals.ts`, `/api/finance/goals`): progress from real balances, monthly need, and the forecast check for goals on checking + savings. Tracking only.
- Tests (synthetic only): `frontend/tests/cash-forecast.test.cjs`, `cash-forecast-store.test.cjs`, `tax-plan.test.cjs`, `finance-budgets.test.cjs`. Keep real amounts, merchants and names out of tests, logs, PR text and commits.

## Focus habit unlock (2026-11-13)

The Onyx Focus iPhone build (`tools/screentime_ios`, `project-focus.yml`; spec and limits in `tools/screentime_ios/FOCUS.md`, phone test steps H0 to H6 in `docs/focus-device-test.md`) can unlock its app block from real habits. Riley picks a few habits and a rule on the `/install` card "Focus: unlock with habits" (`components/FocusUnlockSettings.tsx`; each change autosaves, no Save button): **habits OR time** (finishing them unlocks early; the phone's unlock time unlocks regardless) or **habits AND time** (stays blocked after the unlock time until they are done). Default = nothing chosen = the phone's time-only rule, unchanged. Storage: `pds.focus_unlock_config` (`sql/focus_unlock_config.sql`; one row, `rule` + `habit_page_ids` = `habit_name_map.notion_page_id`, service-role only, at most 10). Routes: `GET/PUT /api/focus/config` (owner session only; PUT is same-origin JSON, at most 1 KB, ids must exist in `habit_name_map`) and **`GET /api/focus/unlock-status`, one more exact path in `isCiRoute()` (never widen it to `/api/focus/*`)**: read-only, answers the behavioral day (`pds.behavioral_today_now()`), the rule and, for the chosen habits only, name + whether `habit_journal` has `answer='Yes'` for that day. Decision table: `shouldUnlock` in `lib/focus-unlock.ts`, mirrored in `FocusCore/Sources/FocusCore/OnyxHabits.swift` (a node test fails if the two drift). **The phone checks only when the app is opened, on "Check now", or from the "Check Onyx Focus habits" shortcut action; there is no background check and no push**, so a finished habit unlocks nothing until then, and under AND the app must be opened once a day. A check that fails (offline, token refused, server error) falls back to the unlock time alone under both rules and says so on the Focus screen; an answer only counts when Onyx's behavioral day equals the phone's calendar day (so from 06:00). Not an ingestion source: no heartbeat, no `/status` card. Tests: `frontend/tests/focus-unlock.test.cjs`, `swift test` in `FocusCore` (run by `focus-ios.yml`). After a change to the Swift side, run `gh workflow run focus-ios-signed.yml` so `/install` offers the new build.
